enforcement.\n139\t    // A tenant impersonation target must come from the gateway's verified\n140\t    // assertion, or from the HMAC-bound effective tenant on auth's direct\n141\t    // JWT-verification path.\n142\t    //\n143\t    // SECURITY (H-13): Cross-tenant access is mandatory audit-logged.\n144\t    // ---------------------------------------------------------------\n145\t    if (this.isSuperAdmin(user)) {\n146\t      const actAs = this.resolveTrustedActAs(request, user);\n147\t      if (actAs) {\n148\t        const isCrossTenant = actAs.targetTenantId !== actAs.sourceTenantId;\n149\t\n150\t        // SECURITY (H-13): MFA step-up enforcement for cross-tenant access\n151\t        if (isCrossTenant) {\n152\t          this.enforceMfaStepUp(actAs.mfaVerified);\n153\t        }\n154\t\n155\t        // SECURITY (H-13 + BULGU-4): Persistent audit logging for cross-tenant access.\n156\t        // Critical security events MUST be awaited to prevent silent audit loss.\n157\t        if (isCrossTenant) {\n158\t          await this.auditCrossTenantAccess(\n159\t            request,\n160\t            user,\n161\t            actAs.sourceTenantId,\n162\t            actAs.targetTenantId,\n163\t            actAs.clientIp,\n164\t            actAs.clientUserAgent,\n165\t            actAs.mfaVerified,\n166\t          );\n167\t        }\n168\t\n169\t        // Publish the tenant context only after all mandatory security checks\n170\t        // and the production audit append have succeeded.\n171\t        request.tenantId = actAs.targetTenantId;\n172\t      }\n173\t      return true;\n174\t    }\n175\t\n176\t    // ---------------------------------------------------------------\n177\t    // Regular users: tenant ID comes EXCLUSIVELY from the JWT claim.\n178\t    // Headers, query params, and body are never consulted.\n179\t    // ---------------------------------------------------------------\n180\t    const tenantId = user?.tenantId;\n181\t\n182\t    if (!tenantId) {\n183\t      throw new BadRequestException(\n184\t        'Tenant ID is required. The JWT must contain a valid tenantId claim.',\n185\t      );\n186\t    }\n187\t\n188\t    if (!UUID_REGEX.test(tenantId)) {\n189\t      throw new BadRequestException('Tenant ID must be a valid UUID');\n190\t    }\n191\t\n192\t    // Store validated tenant ID in request for downstream consumers\n193\t    request.tenantId = tenantId;\n194\t\n195\t    return true;\n196\t  }\n197\t\n198\t  /**\n199\t   * Check if the user has SUPER_ADMIN role.\n200\t   * Supports both the `roles` array and the deprecated `role` string field.\n201\t   */\n202\t  private isSuperAdmin(user?: TenantRequest['user']): boolean {\n203\t    if (!user) return false;\n204\t    if (user.roles?.includes(Role.SUPER_ADMIN)) return true;\n205\t    if (user.role === Role.SUPER_ADMIN) return true;\n206\t    return false;\n207\t  }\n208\t\n209\t  /**\n210\t   * Enforce MFA step-up for SUPER_ADMIN cross-tenant access.\n211\t   *\n212\t   * When the environment variable MFA_REQUIRED_FOR_CROSS_TENANT is set to 'true',\n213\t   * the JWT must contain `mfaVerified: true` for the request to proceed.\n214\t   * This prevents a compromised SUPER_ADMIN session (without MFA) from\n215\t   * accessing other tenants' data.\n216\t   *\n217\t   * @throws ForbiddenException if MFA is required but not verified\n218\t   */\n219\t  private enforceMfaStepUp(mfaVerified: boolean): void {\n220\t    if (!this.mfaRequiredForCrossTenant) {\n221\t      return;\n222\t    }\n223\t\n224\t    if (!mfaVerified) {\n225\t      this.logger.warn(\n226\t        JSON.stringify({\n227\t          event: 'super_admin_cross_tenant_access_denied',\n228\t          reason: 'mfa_not_verified',\n229\t        }),\n230\t      );\n231\t      throw new ForbiddenException(\n232\t        'MFA verification is required for cross-tenant access. ' +\n233\t        'Please complete MFA step-up authentication before accessing another tenant.',\n234\t      );\n235\t    }\n236\t  }\n237\t\n238\t  /**\n239\t   * Persist an audit record for SUPER_ADMIN cross-tenant access.\n240\t   *\n241\t   * SECURITY (H-13 + BULGU-4): This method uses `recordAwait()` so the\n242\t   * audit write is awaited before the request proceeds. Cross-tenant access\n243\t   * by a SUPER_ADMIN is a critical security event — fire-and-forget is\n244\t   * unacceptable because a silent DB failure would leave no forensic trail.\n245\t   *\n246\t   * The record includes:\n247\t   * - userId: the SUPER_ADMIN performing the action\n248\t   * - sourceTenantId: the admin's own tenant (or 'system')\n249\t   * - targetTenantId: the tenant being accessed\n250\t   * - endpoint: HTTP method + URL\n251\t   * - timestamp: ISO 8601 timestamp\n252\t   * - client IP and user agent for forensic traceability\n253\t   *\n254\t   * Production fails closed if the service is missing or the append fails;\n255\t   * otherwise a privileged mutation could succeed without a forensic record.\n256\t   * Non-production keeps an ephemeral fallback for isolated unit/E2E harnesses.\n257\t   */\n258\t  private async auditCrossTenantAccess(\n259\t    request: TenantRequest,\n260\t    user: TenantRequest['user'],\n261\t    sourceTenantId: string,\n262\t    targetTenantId: string,\n263\t    assertedClientIp: string | undefined,\n264\t    assertedClientUserAgent: string | undefined,\n265\t    mfaVerified: boolean,\n266\t  ): Promise<void> {\n267\t    const endpoint = `${request.method} ${request.url}`;\n268\t    const timestamp = new Date().toISOString();\n269\t    const ip = assertedClientIp ?? this.extractClientIp(request);\n270\t    const userAgent =\n271\t      assertedClientUserAgent ??\n272\t      (typeof request.headers['user-agent'] === 'string'\n273\t        ? request.headers['user-agent']\n274\t        : undefined);\n275\t\n276\t    // Always emit an ephemeral log for real-time observability\n277\t    this.logger.warn(\n278\t      JSON.stringify({\n279\t        event: 'super_admin_cross_tenant_access',\n280\t        mfaVerified,\n281\t      }),\n282\t    );\n283\t\n284\t    // Persist to audit trail via AuditLogService (awaited for critical events)\n285\t    if (!this.auditLogService) {\n286\t      if (this.isProduction) {\n287\t        throw new ServiceUnavailableException(\n288\t          'Cross-tenant audit trail is unavailable',\n289\t        );\n290\t      }\n291\t      return;\n292\t    }\n293\t\n294\t    try {\n295\t      await this.auditLogService.recordAwait({\n296\t        action: 'SUPER_ADMIN_CROSS_TENANT_ACCESS',\n297\t        resource: 'TenantGuard',\n298\t        resourceId: targetTenantId,\n299\t        userId: user?.sub ?? null,\n300\t        userEmail: user?.email ?? null,\n301\t        tenantId: targetTenantId,\n302\t        metadata: {\n303\t          sourceTenantId,\n304\t          targetTenantId,\n305\t          endpoint,\n306\t          timestamp,\n307\t          mfaVerified,\n308\t        },\n309\t        actorHomeTenantId:\n310\t          sourceTenantId === 'system' ? null : sourceTenantId,\n311\t        actedOnTenantId: targetTenantId,\n312\t        mfaVerified,\n313\t        ip: ip ?? null,\n314\t        userAgent: userAgent ?? null,\n315\t        severity: AuditSeverity.WARNING,\n316\t      });\n317\t    } catch (err: unknown) {\n318\t      this.logger.error(\n319\t        JSON.stringify({\n320\t          event: 'super_admin_cross_tenant_audit_failed',\n321\t          errorType: err instanceof Error ? err.name : 'UnknownError',\n322\t        }),\n323\t      );\n324\t      if (this.isProduction) {\n325\t        throw new ServiceUnavailableException(\n326\t          'Cross-tenant audit trail is unavailable',\n327\t        );\n328\t      }\n329\t    }\n330\t  }\n331\t\n332\t  /**\n333\t   * Resolve the single trusted SUPER_ADMIN tenant target.\n334\t   *\n335\t   * The verified assertion is authoritative in regular subgraphs. Auth-service\n336\t   * verifies the access JWT itself, so it consumes the HMAC-bound effective\n337\t   * tenant from the verified gateway service identity. Raw browser headers are\n338\t   * never trusted in production.\n339\t   */\n340\t  private resolveTrustedActAs(\n341\t    request: TenantRequest,\n342\t    user: TenantRequest['user'],\n343\t  ):\n344\t    | {\n345\t        sourceTenantId: string;\n346\t        targetTenantId: string;\n347\t        mfaVerified: boolean;\n348\t        clientIp?: string;\n349\t        clientUserAgent?: string;\n350\t      }\n351\t    | undefined {\n352\t    const assertion = request.verifiedUserAssertion;\n353\t    const rawActAs = this.extractActAsTenantHeader(request);\n354\t\n355\t    if (assertion) {\n356\t      if (\n357\t        assertion.issuer !== 'gateway-api' ||\n358\t        assertion.subject !== user?.sub ||\n359\t        !assertion.roles.includes(Role.SUPER_ADMIN)\n360\t      ) {\n361\t        throw new ForbiddenException('Verified user assertion does not match authenticated user');\n362\t      }\n363\t\n364\t      const targetTenantId = assertion.effectiveTenantId ?? undefined;\n365\t      if (!targetTenantId) {\n366\t        if (rawActAs) {\n367\t          throw new ForbiddenException('Act-as tenant conflicts with verified user assertion');\n368\t        }\n369\t        return undefined;\n370\t      }\n371\t      this.assertValidActAsTenant(targetTenantId);\n372\t      if (rawActAs && rawActAs !== targetTenantId) {\n373\t        throw new ForbiddenException('Act-as tenant conflicts with verified user assertion');\n374\t      }\n375\t      this.assertGatewayIdentityMatchesTarget(request, targetTenantId);\n376\t\n377\t      return {\n378\t        sourceTenantId: assertion.tenantId ?? 'system',\n379\t        targetTenantId,\n380\t        mfaVerified: assertion.mfaVerified,\n381\t        clientIp: assertion.clientIp ?? undefined,\n382\t        clientUserAgent: assertion.clientUserAgent ?? undefined,\n383\t      };\n384\t    }\n385\t\n386\t    const identityTarget =\n387\t      request.verifiedIdentity?.serviceName === 'gateway-api'\n388\t        ? request.verifiedIdentity.effectiveTenantId\n389\t        : undefined;\n390\t    if (identityTarget) {\n391\t      this.assertValidActAsTenant(identityTarget);\n392\t      if (rawActAs && rawActAs !== identityTarget) {\n393\t        throw new ForbiddenException(\n394\t          'Act-as tenant conflicts with verified gateway identity',\n395\t        );\n396\t      }\n397\t      this.assertGatewayIdentityMatchesTarget(request, identityTarget);\n398\t      return {\n399\t        sourceTenantId: user?.tenantId ?? 'system',\n400\t        targetTenantId: identityTarget,\n401\t        mfaVerified: user?.mfaVerified === true,\n402\t      };\n403\t    }\n404\t\n405\t    if (!rawActAs) {\n406\t      return undefined;\n407\t    }\n408\t\n409\t    this.assertValidActAsTenant(rawActAs);\n410\t    this.assertGatewayIdentityMatchesTarget(request, rawActAs);\n411\t    return {\n412\t      sourceTenantId: user?.tenantId ?? 'system',\n413\t      targetTenantId: rawActAs,\n414\t      mfaVerified: user?.mfaVerified === true,\n415\t    };\n416\t  }\n417\t\n418\t  private assertValidActAsTenant(tenantId: string): void {\n419\t    if (!UUID_REGEX.test(tenantId)) {\n420\t      throw new BadRequestException(\n421\t        'X-Act-As-Tenant header must be a valid UUID',\n422\t      );\n423\t    }\n424\t  }\n425\t\n426\t  private assertGatewayIdentityMatchesTarget(\n427\t    request: TenantRequest,\n428\t    targetTenantId: string,\n429\t  ): void {\n430\t    const identity = request.verifiedIdentity;\n431\t    if (!identity) {\n432\t      if (this.isProduction) {\n433\t        throw new ForbiddenException(\n434\t          'Cross-tenant access requires a verified gateway identity',\n435\t        );\n436\t      }\n437\t      return;\n438\t    }\n439\t\n440\t    if (\n441\t      identity.serviceName !== 'gateway-api' ||\n442\t      identity.tenantId !== targetTenantId ||\n443\t      identity.effectiveTenantId !== targetTenantId\n444\t    ) {\n445\t      throw new ForbiddenException(\n446\t        'Cross-tenant target does not match verified gateway identity',\n447\t      );\n448\t    }\n449\t  }\n450\t\n451\t  /**\n452\t   * Extract the client IP address from the request.\n453\t   *\n454\t   * SECURITY (BULGU-7): Prefer Express `request.ip` which respects the\n455\t   * application-level `trust proxy` configuration. When trust proxy is\n456\t   * properly set, Express parses X-Forwarded-For securely and returns\n457\t   * the correct client IP. Falling back to raw X-Forwarded-For header\n458\t   * only when `request.ip` is unavailable ensures IP spoofing is\n459\t   * prevented in environments where trust proxy is configured.\n460\t   */\n461\t  private extractClientIp(request: TenantRequest): string | undefined {\n462\t    // Prefer Express request.ip which respects trust proxy configuration\n463\t    if (request.ip) {\n464\t      return request.ip;\n465\t    }\n466\t    // Fallback to X-Forwarded-For header\n467\t    const forwarded = request.headers['x-forwarded-for'];\n468\t    if (typeof forwarded === 'string') {\n469\t      return forwarded.split(',')[0]?.trim();\n470\t    }\n471\t    return undefined;\n472\t  }\n473\t\n474\t  /**\n475\t   * Extract the X-Act-As-Tenant header for SUPER_ADMIN tenant impersonation.\n476\t   *\n477\t   * SECURITY (C-04): This is the ONLY mechanism for super admins to specify a\n478\t   * target tenant. The generic X-Tenant-Id header, query params, and request\n479\t   * body are intentionally excluded to maintain a single auditable impersonation\n480\t   * vector and eliminate confusion with attacker-controlled inputs.\n481\t   */\n482\t  private extractActAsTenantHeader(request: TenantRequest): string | undefined {\n483\t    const header = request.headers['x-act-as-tenant'];\n484\t    return typeof header === 'string' ? header : undefined;\n485\t  }\n486\t}\n487\t"}]},"parent_tool_use_id":null,"session_id":"b73213a0-f835-4f7c-b793-eff657206976","uuid":"ed8166f1-71de-44cc-b2ea-e09d4ee03035","timestamp":"2026-09-19T13:44:54.899Z","tool_use_result":{"type":"text","file":{"filePath":"/home/gharunner/actions-runner/_work/aquaculture_platform/aquaculture_platform/aria-worktrees/req-AIR-aria-evidence-judge-725e0f69acd6/libs/backend-common/src/guards/tenant.guard.ts","content":"import {\n  Injectable,\n  CanActivate,\n  ExecutionContext,\n  BadRequestException,\n  ForbiddenException,\n  ServiceUnavailableException,\n  Inject,\n  Logger,\n  Optional,\n} from '@nestjs/common';\nimport { GqlExecutionContext } from '@nestjs/graphql';\nimport { ConfigService } from '@nestjs/config';\nimport { Reflector } from '@nestjs/core';\nimport { SKIP_TENANT_GUARD_KEY, IS_PUBLIC_KEY, Role } from '../decorators/roles.decorator';\nimport { TenantRequest } from '../types/tenant-request.interface';\n// IMPORTANT: import from tokens, NOT from `audit-log.service` / `audit-log.entity`.\n// Importing the service would chain through `audit-log.entity` and fire the\n// `@Entity()` decorator on every backend-common consumer, polluting TypeORM's\n// global metadata storage and surfacing as cross-service drift on services\n// that never opted into audit logging (DEFECT-1, INFRA-CRITICAL-021).\nimport {\n  AUDIT_LOG_SERVICE,\n  AuditSeverity,\n  type IAuditLogService,\n} from '../audit/audit-log.tokens';\n\n/** UUID v4 format validator. */\nconst UUID_REGEX = /^[0-9a-f]{8}-[0-9a-f]{4}-[0-9a-f]{4}-[0-9a-f]{4}-[0-9a-f]{12}$/i;\n\n/**\n * Tenant Guard — enforces tenant isolation for every authenticated request.\n *\n * SECURITY (C-04): Tenant ID sources have been reduced to the minimum trusted set:\n *\n *   **Regular users** — The ONLY accepted source is `req.user.tenantId`, decoded\n *   from a cryptographically verified JWT by JwtAuthGuard. Query parameters,\n *   request body, and ALL headers (including X-Tenant-Id) are intentionally\n *   excluded. An authenticated user can trivially set any of those values, so\n *   accepting them would allow tenant context spoofing.\n *\n *   **SUPER_ADMIN** — May impersonate a specific tenant only through the\n *   gateway-resolved, HMAC-bound effective-tenant context. Regular subgraphs\n *   consume the verified user assertion; auth-service consumes the verified\n *   gateway service identity because it verifies the user's JWT itself.\n *\n * Skip behaviour:\n * - `@SkipTenantGuard()` skips tenant validation for a single route that\n *   still requires authentication.\n * - `@Public()` marks the endpoint as publicly accessible; TenantGuard\n *   checks both `skipTenantGuard` and `isPublic` metadata so either\n *   decorator is sufficient to bypass tenant validation.\n *\n * SECURITY (H-13): SUPER_ADMIN cross-tenant access is **persistently** audit-logged\n * via AuditLogService with action `SUPER_ADMIN_CROSS_TENANT_ACCESS`. The audit record\n * includes userId, sourceTenantId, targetTenantId, endpoint, timestamp, client IP,\n * and user agent. An ephemeral logger.warn() is also emitted for real-time observability.\n *\n * MFA Step-Up: SUPER_ADMIN cross-tenant access requires `mfaVerified: true` in the JWT\n * claims by default (`MFA_REQUIRED_FOR_CROSS_TENANT` defaults to `'true'`).\n * Set `MFA_REQUIRED_FOR_CROSS_TENANT=false` to opt out during MFA rollout.\n */\n@Injectable()\nexport class TenantGuard implements CanActivate {\n  private readonly logger = new Logger(TenantGuard.name);\n  private readonly isProduction = process.env['NODE_ENV'] === 'production';\n\n  /** Whether MFA step-up is required for SUPER_ADMIN cross-tenant access. */\n  private readonly mfaRequiredForCrossTenant: boolean;\n\n  // WHY: Explicit @Inject() — design:paramtypes may not survive all build/runtime environments\n  // (Alpine musl, prod-only deps). Belt-and-suspenders for NestJS DI resolution.\n  // The audit dependency uses the AUDIT_LOG_SERVICE token + IAuditLogService\n  // interface (NOT the concrete class) so this guard never imports the\n  // `AuditLogEntity` decorator, even transitively. Services that wire\n  // `AuditLogModule.forRoot()` provide the token. Production cross-tenant\n  // requests fail closed if that mandatory append capability is unavailable.\n  constructor(\n    @Inject(Reflector) private reflector: Reflector,\n    @Optional() @Inject(AUDIT_LOG_SERVICE) private readonly auditLogService?: IAuditLogService,\n    @Optional() @Inject(ConfigService) private readonly configService?: ConfigService,\n  ) {\n    this.mfaRequiredForCrossTenant =\n      this.configService?.get<string>('MFA_REQUIRED_FOR_CROSS_TENANT', 'true') !== 'false';\n\n    if (!this.auditLogService) {\n      this.logger.warn(\n        JSON.stringify({\n          event: 'cross_tenant_audit_capability_unavailable',\n          productionFailClosed: this.isProduction,\n        }),\n      );\n    }\n  }\n\n  /**\n   * Evaluate tenant isolation rules for the current request.\n   *\n   * SECURITY (ONEMLI-05): Signature is `async` because cross-tenant audit\n   * logging for SUPER_ADMIN must be awaited to guarantee persistence of\n   * critical security events. NestJS guards fully support both sync\n   * (`boolean`) and async (`Promise<boolean>`) return types.\n   */\n  async canActivate(context: ExecutionContext): Promise<boolean> {\n    // Skip if endpoint is marked public\n    const isPublic = this.reflector.getAllAndOverride<boolean>(\n      IS_PUBLIC_KEY,\n      [context.getHandler(), context.getClass()],\n    );\n\n    if (isPublic) {\n      return true;\n    }\n\n    // Skip if explicitly annotated with @SkipTenantGuard()\n    const skipGuard = this.reflector.getAllAndOverride<boolean>(\n      SKIP_TENANT_GUARD_KEY,\n      [context.getHandler(), context.getClass()],\n    );\n\n    if (skipGuard) {\n      return true;\n    }\n\n    const contextType = context.getType<string>();\n    let request: TenantRequest;\n\n    if (contextType === 'graphql') {\n      const gqlCtx = GqlExecutionContext.create(context);\n      request = gqlCtx.getContext().req as TenantRequest;\n    } else {\n      request = context.switchToHttp().getRequest<TenantRequest>();\n    }\n\n    const user = request.user;\n\n    // ---------------------------------------------------------------\n    // SUPER_ADMIN: operates in system scope, no tenant enforcement.\n    // A tenant impersonation target must come from the gateway's verified\n    // assertion, or from the HMAC-bound effective tenant on auth's direct\n    // JWT-verification path.\n    //\n    // SECURITY (H-13): Cross-tenant access is mandatory audit-logged.\n    // ---------------------------------------------------------------\n    if (this.isSuperAdmin(user)) {\n      const actAs = this.resolveTrustedActAs(request, user);\n      if (actAs) {\n        const isCrossTenant = actAs.targetTenantId !== actAs.sourceTenantId;\n\n        // SECURITY (H-13): MFA step-up enforcement for cross-tenant access\n        if (isCrossTenant) {\n          this.enforceMfaStepUp(actAs.mfaVerified);\n        }\n\n        // SECURITY (H-13 + BULGU-4): Persistent audit logging for cross-tenant access.\n        // Critical security events MUST be awaited to prevent silent audit loss.\n        if (isCrossTenant) {\n          await this.auditCrossTenantAccess(\n            request,\n            user,\n            actAs.sourceTenantId,\n            actAs.targetTenantId,\n            actAs.clientIp,\n            actAs.clientUserAgent,\n            actAs.mfaVerified,\n          );\n        }\n\n        // Publish the tenant context only after all mandatory security checks\n        // and the production audit append have succeeded.\n        request.tenantId = actAs.targetTenantId;\n      }\n      return true;\n    }\n\n    // ---------------------------------------------------------------\n    // Regular users: tenant ID comes EXCLUSIVELY from the JWT claim.\n    // Headers, query params, and body are never consulted.\n    // ---------------------------------------------------------------\n    const tenantId = user?.tenantId;\n\n    if (!tenantId) {\n      throw new BadRequestException(\n        'Tenant ID is required. The JWT must contain a valid tenantId claim.',\n      );\n    }\n\n    if (!UUID_REGEX.test(tenantId)) {\n      throw new BadRequestException('Tenant ID must be a valid UUID');\n    }\n\n    // Store validated tenant ID in request for downstream consumers\n    request.tenantId = tenantId;\n\n    return true;\n  }\n\n  /**\n   * Check if the user has SUPER_ADMIN role.\n   * Supports both the `roles` array and the deprecated `role` string field.\n   */\n  private isSuperAdmin(user?: TenantRequest['user']): boolean {\n    if (!user) return false;\n    if (user.roles?.includes(Role.SUPER_ADMIN)) return true;\n    if (user.role === Role.SUPER_ADMIN) return true;\n    return false;\n  }\n\n  /**\n   * Enforce MFA step-up for SUPER_ADMIN cross-tenant access.\n   *\n   * When the environment variable MFA_REQUIRED_FOR_CROSS_TENANT is set to 'true',\n   * the JWT must contain `mfaVerified: true` for the request to proceed.\n   * This prevents a compromised SUPER_ADMIN session (without MFA) from\n   * accessing other tenants' data.\n   *\n   * @throws ForbiddenException if MFA is required but not verified\n   */\n  private enforceMfaStepUp(mfaVerified: boolean): void {\n    if (!this.mfaRequiredForCrossTenant) {\n      return;\n    }\n\n    if (!mfaVerified) {\n      this.logger.warn(\n        JSON.stringify({\n          event: 'super_admin_cross_tenant_access_denied',\n          reason: 'mfa_not_verified',\n        }),\n      );\n      throw new ForbiddenException(\n        'MFA verification is required for cross-tenant access. ' +\n        'Please complete MFA step-up authentication before accessing another tenant.',\n      );\n    }\n  }\n\n  /**\n   * Persist an audit record for SUPER_ADMIN cross-tenant access.\n   *\n   * SECURITY (H-13 + BULGU-4): This method uses `recordAwait()` so the\n   * audit write is awaited before the request proceeds. Cross-tenant access\n   * by a SUPER_ADMIN is a critical security event — fire-and-forget is\n   * unacceptable because a silent DB failure would leave no forensic trail.\n   *\n   * The record includes:\n   * - userId: the SUPER_ADMIN performing the action\n   * - sourceTenantId: the admin's own tenant (or 'system')\n   * - targetTenantId: the tenant being accessed\n   * - endpoint: HTTP method + URL\n   * - timestamp: ISO 8601 timestamp\n   * - client IP and user agent for forensic traceability\n   *\n   * Production fails closed if the service is missing or the append fails;\n   * otherwise a privileged mutation could succeed without a forensic record.\n   * Non-production keeps an ephemeral fallback for isolated unit/E2E harnesses.\n   */\n  private async auditCrossTenantAccess(\n    request: TenantRequest,\n    user: TenantRequest['user'],\n    sourceTenantId: string,\n    targetTenantId: string,\n    assertedClientIp: string | undefined,\n    assertedClientUserAgent: string | undefined,\n    mfaVerified: boolean,\n  ): Promise<void> {\n    const endpoint = `${request.method} ${request.url}`;\n    const timestamp = new Date().toISOString();\n    const ip = assertedClientIp ?? this.extractClientIp(request);\n    const userAgent =\n      assertedClientUserAgent ??\n      (typeof request.headers['user-agent'] === 'string'\n        ? request.headers['user-agent']\n        : undefined);\n\n    // Always emit an ephemeral log for real-time observability\n    this.logger.warn(\n      JSON.stringify({\n        event: 'super_admin_cross_tenant_access',\n        mfaVerified,\n      }),\n    );\n\n    // Persist to audit trail via AuditLogService (awaited for critical events)\n    if (!this.auditLogService) {\n      if (this.isProduction) {\n        throw new ServiceUnavailableException(\n          'Cross-tenant audit trail is unavailable',\n        );\n      }\n      return;\n    }\n\n    try {\n      await this.auditLogService.recordAwait({\n        action: 'SUPER_ADMIN_CROSS_TENANT_ACCESS',\n        resource: 'TenantGuard',\n        resourceId: targetTenantId,\n        userId: user?.sub ?? null,\n        userEmail: user?.email ?? null,\n        tenantId: targetTenantId,\n        metadata: {\n          sourceTenantId,\n          targetTenantId,\n          endpoint,\n          timestamp,\n          mfaVerified,\n        },\n        actorHomeTenantId:\n          sourceTenantId === 'system' ? null : sourceTenantId,\n        actedOnTenantId: targetTenantId,\n        mfaVerified,\n        ip: ip ?? null,\n        userAgent: userAgent ?? null,\n        severity: AuditSeverity.WARNING,\n      });\n    } catch (err: unknown) {\n      this.logger.error(\n        JSON.stringify({\n          event: 'super_admin_cross_tenant_audit_failed',\n          errorType: err instanceof Error ? err.name : 'UnknownError',\n        }),\n      );\n      if (this.isProduction) {\n        throw new ServiceUnavailableException(\n          'Cross-tenant audit trail is unavailable',\n        );\n      }\n    }\n  }\n\n  /**\n   * Resolve the single trusted SUPER_ADMIN tenant target.\n   *\n   * The verified assertion is authoritative in regular subgraphs. Auth-service\n   * verifies the access JWT itself, so it consumes the HMAC-bound effective\n   * tenant from the verified gateway service identity. Raw browser headers are\n   * never trusted in production.\n   */\n  private resolveTrustedActAs(\n    request: TenantRequest,\n    user: TenantRequest['user'],\n  ):\n    | {\n        sourceTenantId: string;\n        targetTenantId: string;\n        mfaVerified: boolean;\n        clientIp?: string;\n        clientUserAgent?: string;\n      }\n    | undefined {\n    const assertion = request.verifiedUserAssertion;\n    const rawActAs = this.extractActAsTenantHeader(request);\n\n    if (assertion) {\n      if (\n        assertion.issuer !== 'gateway-api' ||\n        assertion.subject !== user?.sub ||\n        !assertion.roles.includes(Role.SUPER_ADMIN)\n      ) {\n        throw new ForbiddenException('Verified user assertion does not match authenticated user');\n      }\n\n      const targetTenantId = assertion.effectiveTenantId ?? undefined;\n      if (!targetTenantId) {\n        if (rawActAs) {\n          throw new ForbiddenException('Act-as tenant conflicts with verified user assertion');\n        }\n        return undefined;\n      }\n      this.assertValidActAsTenant(targetTenantId);\n      if (rawActAs && rawActAs !== targetTenantId) {\n        throw new ForbiddenException('Act-as tenant conflicts with verified user assertion');\n      }\n      this.assertGatewayIdentityMatchesTarget(request, targetTenantId);\n\n      return {\n        sourceTenantId: assertion.tenantId ?? 'system',\n        targetTenantId,\n        mfaVerified: assertion.mfaVerified,\n        clientIp: assertion.clientIp ?? undefined,\n        clientUserAgent: assertion.clientUserAgent ?? undefined,\n      };\n    }\n\n    const identityTarget =\n      request.verifiedIdentity?.serviceName === 'gateway-api'\n        ? request.verifiedIdentity.effectiveTenantId\n        : undefined;\n    if (identityTarget) {\n      this.assertValidActAsTenant(identityTarget);\n      if (rawActAs && rawActAs !== identityTarget) {\n        throw new ForbiddenException(\n          'Act-as tenant conflicts with verified gateway identity',\n        );\n      }\n      this.assertGatewayIdentityMatchesTarget(request, identityTarget);\n      return {\n        sourceTenantId: user?.tenantId ?? 'system',\n        targetTenantId: identityTarget,\n        mfaVerified: user?.mfaVerified === true,\n      };\n    }\n\n    if (!rawActAs) {\n      return undefined;\n    }\n\n    this.assertValidActAsTenant(rawActAs);\n    this.assertGatewayIdentityMatchesTarget(request, rawActAs);\n    return {\n      sourceTenantId: user?.tenantId ?? 'system',\n      targetTenantId: rawActAs,\n      mfaVerified: user?.mfaVerified === true,\n    };\n  }\n\n  private assertValidActAsTenant(tenantId: string): void {\n    if (!UUID_REGEX.test(tenantId)) {\n      throw new BadRequestException(\n        'X-Act-As-Tenant header must be a valid UUID',\n      );\n    }\n  }\n\n  private assertGatewayIdentityMatchesTarget(\n    request: TenantRequest,\n    targetTenantId: string,\n  ): void {\n    const identity = request.verifiedIdentity;\n    if (!identity) {\n      if (this.isProduction) {\n        throw new ForbiddenException(\n          'Cross-tenant access requires a verified gateway identity',\n        );\n      }\n      return;\n    }\n\n    if (\n      identity.serviceName !== 'gateway-api' ||\n      identity.tenantId !== targetTenantId ||\n      identity.effectiveTenantId !== targetTenantId\n    ) {\n      throw new ForbiddenException(\n        'Cross-tenant target does not match verified gateway identity',\n      );\n    }\n  }\n\n  /**\n   * Extract the client IP address from the request.\n   *\n   * SECURITY (BULGU-7): Prefer Express `request.ip` which respects the\n   * application-level `trust proxy` configuration. When trust proxy is\n   * properly set, Express parses X-Forwarded-For securely and returns\n   * the correct client IP. Falling back to raw X-Forwarded-For header\n   * only when `request.ip` is unavailable ensures IP spoofing is\n   * prevented in environments where trust proxy is configured.\n   */\n  private extractClientIp(request: TenantRequest): string | undefined {\n    // Prefer Express request.ip which respects trust proxy configuration\n    if (request.ip) {\n      return request.ip;\n    }\n    // Fallback to X-Forwarded-For header\n    const forwarded = request.headers['x-forwarded-for'];\n    if (typeof forwarded === 'string') {\n      return forwarded.split(',')[0]?.trim();\n    }\n    return undefined;\n  }\n\n  /**\n   * Extract the X-Act-As-Tenant header for SUPER_ADMIN tenant impersonation.\n   *\n   * SECURITY (C-04): This is the ONLY mechanism for super admins to specify a\n   * target tenant. The generic X-Tenant-Id header, query params, and request\n   * body are intentionally excluded to maintain a single auditable impersonation\n   * vector and eliminate confusion with attacker-controlled inputs.\n   */\n  private extractActAsTenantHeader(request: TenantRequest): string | undefined {\n    const header = request.headers['x-act-as-tenant'];\n    return typeof header === 'string' ? header : undefined;\n  }\n}\n","numLines":487,"startLine":1,"totalLines":487}}}

{"type":"user","message":{"role":"user","content":[{"tool_use_id":"toolu_01EePymq617tnm6GbktZdEPp","type":"tool_result","content":"1\timport {\n2\t  enforceAccessTokenType,\n3\t  enforceTokenNotRevoked,\n4\t  getJwtVerifyOptions,\n5\t} from '@aquaculture/backend-common/auth';\n6\timport { requestContextStorage } from '@aquaculture/backend-common/logging';\n7\timport {\n8\t  IpRateLimiterService,\n9\t  IUserTokenRevocation,\n10\t  SecurityEventService,\n11\t  TOKEN_BLACKLIST,\n12\t  USER_TOKEN_REVOCATION,\n13\t  type ITokenBlacklist,\n14\t} from '@aquaculture/backend-common/security';\n15\timport {\n16\t  CanActivate,\n17\t  ExecutionContext,\n18\t  ForbiddenException,\n19\t  HttpException,\n20\t  HttpStatus,\n21\t  Inject,\n22\t  Injectable,\n23\t  Logger,\n24\t  UnauthorizedException,\n25\t} from '@nestjs/common';\n26\timport { ConfigService } from '@nestjs/config';\n27\timport { Reflector } from '@nestjs/core';\n28\timport { JwtService } from '@nestjs/jwt';\n29\timport { toPlatformCapabilities } from '@platform/event-contracts';\n30\timport * as jwt from 'jsonwebtoken';\n31\t\n32\timport { ROLES_KEY } from '../decorators/roles.decorator';\n33\t// APA-371: read the platform-SSoT bypass key (re-exported from backend-common)\n34\t// instead of declaring a local 'isPublic' copy the guard must keep in sync.\n35\timport { IS_PUBLIC_KEY } from '../decorators/public.decorator';\n36\t// Bind the WRITER to the canonical request-user SSoT: AuthenticatedUser extends\n37\t// JwtUser, so `request.user = { ... }` below fails type-check if it omits `sub`\n38\t// (ORPHAN-146). The shared ThrottlerGuard reads that same `sub`.\n39\timport { AuthenticatedRequest } from '../shared/authenticated-request';\n40\t\n41\t// WHY: Explicit @Inject() required — useClass + APP_GUARD relies on design:paramtypes\n42\t// metadata which may not survive all build/runtime environments (Alpine musl, prod-only deps).\n43\t// getJwtVerifyOptions: centralised JWT verification options (RS256 public\n44\t// key, issuer, audience).\n45\t// BEFORE: guard used `import * as jwt from 'jsonwebtoken'` with jwt.verify() —\n46\t// synchronous (blocks event loop), no algorithm restriction, no issuer/audience check.\n47\t// AFTER: guard uses JwtService.verifyAsync() with getJwtVerifyOptions() — async,\n48\t// algorithm-restricted to RS256 (asymmetric — auth-service signs with the\n49\t// private key, every consumer verifies with the public key), issuer + audience\n50\t// enforced at library level. jsonwebtoken still imported for error\n51\t// type-checking in the catch block.\n52\t\n53\t/**\n54\t * JWT payload structure for admin-api guard.\n55\t *\n56\t * SECURITY (H-08): email is now optional because auth-service no longer\n57\t * embeds PII (email, firstName, lastName) in JWT tokens. Services that\n58\t * need the user's email should fetch it from auth-service using the sub (user ID).\n59\t */\n60\texport interface JwtPayload {\n61\t  sub: string;\n62\t  /** @deprecated Optional -- JWT no longer carries email PII (H-08) */\n63\t  email?: string;\n64\t  role?: string;\n65\t  roles?: string[];\n66\t  tenantId?: string;\n67\t  type?: string;\n68\t  jti?: string;\n69\t  iat: number;\n70\t  exp: number /** MFA step-up cleared on this session (auth-service claim). */;\n71\t  mfaVerified?: boolean;\n72\t  /** ADR-0016: platform-operator capabilities minted from auth.platform_capability_grants. */\n73\t  platformCapabilities?: string[];\n74\t}\n75\t\n76\t// Product language calls this actor \"platform admin\"; the auth domain\n77\t// represents that platform-level operator with the existing SUPER_ADMIN role.\n78\tconst DEFAULT_ADMIN_ROLES = ['SUPER_ADMIN', 'super_admin'];\n79\t\n80\t/** Extract a human-readable reason from an UnauthorizedException for logging + events. */\n81\tfunction reasonOf(exception: UnauthorizedException): string {\n82\t  const response = exception.getResponse();\n83\t  if (typeof response === 'string') return response;\n84\t  if (response && typeof response === 'object' && 'message' in response) {\n85\t    const message = (response as { message?: unknown }).message;\n86\t    if (typeof message === 'string') return message;\n87\t    if (Array.isArray(message)) return message.join(', ');\n88\t  }\n89\t  return exception.message || 'Unauthorized';\n90\t}\n91\t\n92\t/** Normalize a possibly-array header to a single string value. */\n93\tfunction headerValue(value: string | string[] | undefined): string | undefined {\n94\t  return Array.isArray(value) ? value[0] : value;\n95\t}\n96\t\n97\t@Injectable()\n98\texport class PlatformAdminGuard implements CanActivate {\n99\t  private readonly logger = new Logger(PlatformAdminGuard.name);\n100\t\n101\t  constructor(\n102\t    @Inject(Reflector) private readonly reflector: Reflector,\n103\t    @Inject(ConfigService) private readonly configService: ConfigService,\n104\t    @Inject(JwtService) private readonly jwtService: JwtService,\n105\t    // APA-367: admin-api is a directly-reachable auth boundary (prod nginx routes\n106\t    // /api/ straight here, bypassing gateway-api's blacklist-checking guard), so\n107\t    // it MUST self-enforce token revocation. Both stores are REQUIRED (not\n108\t    // @Optional): the @Global TokenBlacklistModule + UserTokenRevocationModule are\n109\t    // registered in app.module, so a missing wiring fails DI at boot rather than\n110\t    // silently disabling revocation on the most privileged surface. In production\n111\t    // TokenBlacklistService itself fails fast unless Redis is configured, so the\n112\t    // check is cross-instance correct.\n113\t    // TOKEN_BLACKLIST's canonical contract exposes isBlacklisted(). Adapt it to\n114\t    // the shared verifier's read-only isValidToken() capability only at the call\n115\t    // boundary below, so every Nest module resolves the same injection token.\n116\t    @Inject(TOKEN_BLACKLIST)\n117\t    private readonly tokenBlacklist: ITokenBlacklist,\n118\t    @Inject(USER_TOKEN_REVOCATION)\n119\t    private readonly userTokenRevocation: IUserTokenRevocation,\n120\t    // APA-369: this guard is the FIRST APP_GUARD, so a request with a\n121\t    // missing/invalid/expired/forged/revoked Bearer is rejected here BEFORE the\n122\t    // shared ThrottlerGuard (registered second) ever runs — token brute-force /\n123\t    // credential-stuffing against the platform-admin API was never app-throttled\n124\t    // and produced no security event. Reordering the guards is unsound (the\n125\t    // ThrottlerGuard reads request.user.sub, which only THIS guard populates, so\n126\t    // throttle-first would re-classify every authenticated SUPER_ADMIN as\n127\t    // anonymous). Instead we account each auth FAILURE against a per-IP bucket\n128\t    // (only failures increment it, so a valid operator's fan-out never counts)\n129\t    // and emit a security event that reaches the incident pipeline.\n130\t    @Inject(IpRateLimiterService)\n131\t    private readonly failedAuthIpLimiter: IpRateLimiterService,\n132\t    @Inject(SecurityEventService)\n133\t    private readonly securityEvents: SecurityEventService,\n134\t  ) {\n135\t    // SECURITY (CRITICAL-001): JWT_SECRET length validation removed in WS2.B\n136\t    // (2026-04-14). The check was a hold-over from the HS256 era — verifyAsync\n137\t    // already uses getJwtVerifyOptions() which loads the RS256 public key and\n138\t    // throws at startup if it is missing. There is no JWT_SECRET to validate\n139\t    // anymore; reading it would also trip the ESLint no-restricted-syntax\n140\t    // ban on JWT_SECRET reads (WS2.C).\n141\t  }\n142\t\n143\t  async canActivate(context: ExecutionContext): Promise<boolean> {\n144\t    // Hybrid-app boundary: this guard authenticates the HTTP surface only.\n145\t    // admin-api-service also consumes NATS events (RPC context) — those are\n146\t    // authenticated by the broker-verified mTLS client-cert CN (ADR-015) and\n147\t    // the per-subject publish ACL (e.g. only farm-service may publish\n148\t    // TenantOnboardingAck), NOT by a Bearer JWT, which does not exist on the\n149\t    // NATS surface. Without this short-circuit the guard would call\n150\t    // switchToHttp().getRequest() on an RPC context and reject every event.\n151\t    if (context.getType() !== 'http') {\n152\t      return true;\n153\t    }\n154\t\n155\t    // Check if route is marked as public\n156\t    const isPublic = this.reflector.getAllAndOverride<boolean>(IS_PUBLIC_KEY, [\n157\t      context.getHandler(),\n158\t      context.getClass(),\n159\t    ]);\n160\t\n161\t    if (isPublic) {\n162\t      return true;\n163\t    }\n164\t\n165\t    const request = context.switchToHttp().getRequest<AuthenticatedRequest>();\n166\t    const authHeader = request.headers.authorization;\n167\t\n168\t    if (!authHeader) {\n169\t      return this.rejectAuth(\n170\t        context,\n171\t        request,\n172\t        new UnauthorizedException('No authorization header provided'),\n173\t      );\n174\t    }\n175\t\n176\t    const [type, token] = authHeader.split(' ');\n177\t\n178\t    if (type !== 'Bearer' || !token) {\n179\t      return this.rejectAuth(\n180\t        context,\n181\t        request,\n182\t        new UnauthorizedException('Invalid authorization header format'),\n183\t      );\n184\t    }\n185\t\n186\t    try {\n187\t      // BEFORE: jwt.verify() — synchronous, no algorithm restriction, no iss/aud validation.\n188\t      // AFTER: JwtService.verifyAsync() with getJwtVerifyOptions() — async (non-blocking),\n189\t      // RS256 algorithm enforced, issuer and audience validated at jsonwebtoken library level.\n190\t      const payload = await this.jwtService.verifyAsync<JwtPayload>(\n191\t        token,\n192\t        getJwtVerifyOptions(this.configService),\n193\t      );\n194\t      enforceAccessTokenType(\n195\t        payload,\n196\t        this.logger,\n197\t        this.configService.get<string>('NODE_ENV') === 'production',\n198\t      );\n199\t\n200\t      // APA-367: mandatory post-verify revocation check. A signature-valid\n201\t      // SUPER_ADMIN token whose session was force-logged-out, whose owner was\n202\t      // deactivated/deleted, or whose password was reset must be rejected here —\n203\t      // otherwise the token keeps working against admin-api until natural expiry,\n204\t      // silently defeating the platform's emergency-cutoff controls. Consults\n205\t      // both revocation namespaces (per-jti/user blacklist + user_blacklist epoch)\n206\t      // via the shared primitive so admin-api cannot drift out of the contract\n207\t      // that gateway-api and auth-service already enforce.\n208\t      await enforceTokenNotRevoked(\n209\t        payload,\n210\t        {\n211\t          tokenBlacklist: {\n212\t            isValidToken: (jti: string): Promise<boolean> =>\n213\t              this.tokenBlacklist.isBlacklisted(jti).then((blacklisted) => !blacklisted),\n214\t          },\n215\t          userTokenRevocation: this.userTokenRevocation,\n216\t        },\n217\t        this.logger,\n218\t      );\n219\t\n220\t      // Normalize user roles - tekil role varsa array'e çevir\n221\t      const userRoles = payload.roles || (payload.role ? [payload.role] : []);\n222\t\n223\t      // Attach user to request first (for later use in controllers).\n224\t      // WHY both `sub` and `id`: the shared backend-common ThrottlerGuard (and\n225\t      // every `@CurrentUser('sub')` consumer) reads the canonical JWT subject\n226\t      // as `request.user.sub`. This guard historically exposed ONLY `id`, so\n227\t      // the throttler saw `user.sub === undefined`, classified every\n228\t      // authenticated SUPER_ADMIN as ANONYMOUS (20 req/60s) AND keyed the bucket\n229\t      // by IP instead of user — a single operator's admin-panel fan-out tripped\n230\t      // 429s. `id` stays as admin-api's local convention (controllers read it).\n231\t      request.user = {\n232\t        sub: payload.sub,\n233\t        id: payload.sub,\n234\t        email: payload.email,\n235\t        roles: userRoles,\n236\t        role: payload.role || userRoles[0],\n237\t        tenantId: payload.tenantId,\n238\t        // Read by AuditedOperationInterceptor for the mandatory mfaVerified column\n239\t        // and by DestructiveActionGuard for the MFA freshness window (ADR-0011).\n240\t        mfaVerified: payload.mfaVerified === true,\n241\t        iat: payload.iat,\n242\t        // ADR-0016: read by PlatformCapabilityGuard (route capability) and\n243\t        // DestructiveActionGuard (break-glass). Narrowed to the closed enum so a\n244\t        // forged or stale string can never act as a capability.\n245\t        platformCapabilities: toPlatformCapabilities(payload.platformCapabilities),\n246\t      };\n247\t\n248\t      const requestContext = requestContextStorage.getStore();\n249\t      if (requestContext) {\n250\t        requestContext.userId = payload.sub;\n251\t        requestContext.tenantId = payload.tenantId;\n252\t        // ADMIN-CRITICAL-102: the audit writer derives the actor from this\n253\t        // frame; a request body can never name who acted.\n254\t        requestContext.userEmail = payload.email;\n255\t        requestContext.mfaVerified = payload.mfaVerified === true;\n256\t      }\n257\t\n258\t      // Admin API is a platform-admin boundary. In the current auth model that\n259\t      // platform actor is encoded as SUPER_ADMIN; decorators may narrow to that\n260\t      // role, but must never widen admin-api access to tenant/module roles.\n261\t      const decoratedRoles = this.reflector.getAllAndOverride<string[]>(ROLES_KEY, [\n262\t        context.getHandler(),\n263\t        context.getClass(),\n264\t      ]);\n265\t      const requiredRoles = (decoratedRoles || DEFAULT_ADMIN_ROLES).filter(\n266\t        (role) => role.toUpperCase() === 'SUPER_ADMIN',\n267\t      );\n268\t      if (requiredRoles.length === 0) {\n269\t        requiredRoles.push('SUPER_ADMIN');\n270\t      }\n271\t\n272\t      // Case-insensitive role check\n273\t      const hasRequiredRole = userRoles.some((userRole) =>\n274\t        requiredRoles.some((required) => required.toUpperCase() === userRole.toUpperCase()),\n275\t      );\n276\t\n277\t      if (!hasRequiredRole) {\n278\t        // SECURITY: Log user ID only -- do not include email PII in logs (H-14)\n279\t        this.logger.warn(\n280\t          `Access denied for userId=${payload.sub}: ` +\n281\t            `has roles [${userRoles.join(', ')}], requires one of [${requiredRoles.join(', ')}]`,\n282\t        );\n283\t        throw new ForbiddenException(`Access denied. Required roles: ${requiredRoles.join(', ')}`);\n284\t      }\n285\t\n286\t      return true;\n287\t    } catch (error) {\n288\t      // A valid token with an insufficient role is NOT an authentication failure\n289\t      // (it is a real, authenticated principal) — rethrow as-is; it must not fill\n290\t      // the failed-auth IP bucket and is already logged at warn above.\n291\t      if (error instanceof ForbiddenException) {\n292\t        throw error;\n293\t      }\n294\t\n295\t      // Rejections from enforceAccessTokenType / enforceTokenNotRevoked (wrong\n296\t      // token type, missing jti, revoked token) — preserve their code by routing\n297\t      // the original exception through the failed-auth accounting.\n298\t      if (error instanceof UnauthorizedException) {\n299\t        return this.rejectAuth(context, request, error);\n300\t      }\n301\t\n302\t      if (error instanceof jwt.TokenExpiredError) {\n303\t        return this.rejectAuth(context, request, new UnauthorizedException('Token has expired'));\n304\t      }\n305\t\n306\t      if (error instanceof jwt.JsonWebTokenError) {\n307\t        return this.rejectAuth(context, request, new UnauthorizedException('Invalid token'));\n308\t      }\n309\t\n310\t      // Unexpected error — keep the stack trace at error level, then account the\n311\t      // failure and reject.\n312\t      this.logger.error(\n313\t        `Authentication error for ${request.method} ${request.url}: ${(error as Error).message}`,\n314\t        (error as Error).stack,\n315\t      );\n316\t      return this.rejectAuth(context, request, new UnauthorizedException('Authentication failed'));\n317\t    }\n318\t  }\n319\t\n320\t  /**\n321\t   * Best-effort client IP: the left-most X-Forwarded-For hop (the original\n322\t   * client through nginx), falling back to the request address. Keyed per-IP so\n323\t   * a failed-auth flood from one source is bounded regardless of the (spoofable)\n324\t   * identity a forged token might claim.\n325\t   */\n326\t  private getClientIp(request: AuthenticatedRequest): string {\n327\t    const forwarded = headerValue(request.headers['x-forwarded-for']);\n328\t    const firstHop = forwarded?.split(',')[0]?.trim();\n329\t    return firstHop || request.ip || 'unknown';\n330\t  }\n331\t\n332\t  /**\n333\t   * APA-369: record a failed authentication against the per-IP bucket, emit the\n334\t   * AUTH_TOKEN_REJECTED security event, and — once an IP crosses the failed-auth\n335\t   * limit — emit RATE_LIMIT_EXCEEDED and reject with 429 (instead of 401) so\n336\t   * token brute-force is APP-throttled (not merely nginx-throttled) and reaches\n337\t   * the incident pipeline. Only auth FAILURES call this, so an authenticated\n338\t   * operator's request fan-out from one IP never fills the bucket.\n339\t   */\n340\t  private async rejectAuth(\n341\t    context: ExecutionContext,\n342\t    request: AuthenticatedRequest,\n343\t    unauthorized: UnauthorizedException,\n344\t  ): Promise<never> {\n345\t    const reason = reasonOf(unauthorized);\n346\t    // WARN, not DEBUG: failed auth on the most privileged surface must be\n347\t    // visible at production log levels.\n348\t    this.logger.warn(`401 Unauthorized: ${reason} for ${request.method} ${request.url}`);\n349\t\n350\t    const ip = this.getClientIp(request);\n351\t    const userAgent = headerValue(request.headers['user-agent']);\n352\t    const limit = this.failedAuthIpLimiter.checkLimit(ip);\n353\t\n354\t    await this.securityEvents.publishTokenRejected({ reason, ip, userAgent });\n355\t\n356\t    if (!limit.allowed) {\n357\t      const windowMs = this.configService.get<number>('IP_RATE_WINDOW_MS', 60000);\n358\t      const maxPerWindow = this.configService.get<number>('IP_RATE_LIMIT', 100);\n359\t      await this.securityEvents.publishRateLimitExceeded({\n360\t        key: `admin-auth:ip:${ip}`,\n361\t        limit: maxPerWindow,\n362\t        windowMs,\n363\t        count: maxPerWindow + 1,\n364\t        ip,\n365\t        userAgent,\n366\t      });\n367\t      const response = context\n368\t        .switchToHttp()\n369\t        .getResponse<{ setHeader(name: string, value: string): void }>();\n370\t      if (limit.retryAfter) {\n371\t        response.setHeader('Retry-After', String(limit.retryAfter));\n372\t      }\n373\t      throw new HttpException(\n374\t        { code: 'TOO_MANY_FAILED_AUTH', message: 'Too many failed authentication attempts' },\n375\t        HttpStatus.TOO_MANY_REQUESTS,\n376\t      );\n377\t    }\n378\t\n379\t    throw unauthorized;\n380\t  }\n381\t}\n382\t"}]},"parent_tool_use_id":null,"session_id":"b73213a0-f835-4f7c-b793-eff657206976","uuid":"953eb5b9-4395-4853-aec6-d9aef6f9c46b","timestamp":"2026-09-19T13:44:55.855Z","tool_use_result":{"type":"text","file":{"filePath":"/home/gharunner/actions-runner/_work/aquaculture_platform/aquaculture_platform/aria-worktrees/req-AIR-aria-evidence-judge-725e0f69acd6/apps/admin-api-service/src/guards/platform-admin.guard.ts","content":"import {\n  enforceAccessTokenType,\n  enforceTokenNotRevoked,\n  getJwtVerifyOptions,\n} from '@aquaculture/backend-common/auth';\nimport { requestContextStorage } from '@aquaculture/backend-common/logging';\nimport {\n  IpRateLimiterService,\n  IUserTokenRevocation,\n  SecurityEventService,\n  TOKEN_BLACKLIST,\n  USER_TOKEN_REVOCATION,\n  type ITokenBlacklist,\n} from '@aquaculture/backend-common/security';\nimport {\n  CanActivate,\n  ExecutionContext,\n  ForbiddenException,\n  HttpException,\n  HttpStatus,\n  Inject,\n  Injectable,\n  Logger,\n  UnauthorizedException,\n} from '@nestjs/common';\nimport { ConfigService } from '@nestjs/config';\nimport { Reflector } from '@nestjs/core';\nimport { JwtService } from '@nestjs/jwt';\nimport { toPlatformCapabilities } from '@platform/event-contracts';\nimport * as jwt from 'jsonwebtoken';\n\nimport { ROLES_KEY } from '../decorators/roles.decorator';\n// APA-371: read the platform-SSoT bypass key (re-exported from backend-common)\n// instead of declaring a local 'isPublic' copy the guard must keep in sync.\nimport { IS_PUBLIC_KEY } from '../decorators/public.decorator';\n// Bind the WRITER to the canonical request-user SSoT: AuthenticatedUser extends\n// JwtUser, so `request.user = { ... }` below fails type-check if it omits `sub`\n// (ORPHAN-146). The shared ThrottlerGuard reads that same `sub`.\nimport { AuthenticatedRequest } from '../shared/authenticated-request';\n\n// WHY: Explicit @Inject() required — useClass + APP_GUARD relies on design:paramtypes\n// metadata which may not survive all build/runtime environments (Alpine musl, prod-only deps).\n// getJwtVerifyOptions: centralised JWT verification options (RS256 public\n// key, issuer, audience).\n// BEFORE: guard used `import * as jwt from 'jsonwebtoken'` with jwt.verify() —\n// synchronous (blocks event loop), no algorithm restriction, no issuer/audience check.\n// AFTER: guard uses JwtService.verifyAsync() with getJwtVerifyOptions() — async,\n// algorithm-restricted to RS256 (asymmetric — auth-service signs with the\n// private key, every consumer verifies with the public key), issuer + audience\n// enforced at library level. jsonwebtoken still imported for error\n// type-checking in the catch block.\n\n/**\n * JWT payload structure for admin-api guard.\n *\n * SECURITY (H-08): email is now optional because auth-service no longer\n * embeds PII (email, firstName, lastName) in JWT tokens. Services that\n * need the user's email should fetch it from auth-service using the sub (user ID).\n */\nexport interface JwtPayload {\n  sub: string;\n  /** @deprecated Optional -- JWT no longer carries email PII (H-08) */\n  email?: string;\n  role?: string;\n  roles?: string[];\n  tenantId?: string;\n  type?: string;\n  jti?: string;\n  iat: number;\n  exp: number /** MFA step-up cleared on this session (auth-service claim). */;\n  mfaVerified?: boolean;\n  /** ADR-0016: platform-operator capabilities minted from auth.platform_capability_grants. */\n  platformCapabilities?: string[];\n}\n\n// Product language calls this actor \"platform admin\"; the auth domain\n// represents that platform-level operator with the existing SUPER_ADMIN role.\nconst DEFAULT_ADMIN_ROLES = ['SUPER_ADMIN', 'super_admin'];\n\n/** Extract a human-readable reason from an UnauthorizedException for logging + events. */\nfunction reasonOf(exception: UnauthorizedException): string {\n  const response = exception.getResponse();\n  if (typeof response === 'string') return response;\n  if (response && typeof response === 'object' && 'message' in response) {\n    const message = (response as { message?: unknown }).message;\n    if (typeof message === 'string') return message;\n    if (Array.isArray(message)) return message.join(', ');\n  }\n  return exception.message || 'Unauthorized';\n}\n\n/** Normalize a possibly-array header to a single string value. */\nfunction headerValue(value: string | string[] | undefined): string | undefined {\n  return Array.isArray(value) ? value[0] : value;\n}\n\n@Injectable()\nexport class PlatformAdminGuard implements CanActivate {\n  private readonly logger = new Logger(PlatformAdminGuard.name);\n\n  constructor(\n    @Inject(Reflector) private readonly reflector: Reflector,\n    @Inject(ConfigService) private readonly configService: ConfigService,\n    @Inject(JwtService) private readonly jwtService: JwtService,\n    // APA-367: admin-api is a directly-reachable auth boundary (prod nginx routes\n    // /api/ straight here, bypassing gateway-api's blacklist-checking guard), so\n    // it MUST self-enforce token revocation. Both stores are REQUIRED (not\n    // @Optional): the @Global TokenBlacklistModule + UserTokenRevocationModule are\n    // registered in app.module, so a missing wiring fails DI at boot rather than\n    // silently disabling revocation on the most privileged surface. In production\n    // TokenBlacklistService itself fails fast unless Redis is configured, so the\n    // check is cross-instance correct.\n    // TOKEN_BLACKLIST's canonical contract exposes isBlacklisted(). Adapt it to\n    // the shared verifier's read-only isValidToken() capability only at the call\n    // boundary below, so every Nest module resolves the same injection token.\n    @Inject(TOKEN_BLACKLIST)\n    private readonly tokenBlacklist: ITokenBlacklist,\n    @Inject(USER_TOKEN_REVOCATION)\n    private readonly userTokenRevocation: IUserTokenRevocation,\n    // APA-369: this guard is the FIRST APP_GUARD, so a request with a\n    // missing/invalid/expired/forged/revoked Bearer is rejected here BEFORE the\n    // shared ThrottlerGuard (registered second) ever runs — token brute-force /\n    // credential-stuffing against the platform-admin API was never app-throttled\n    // and produced no security event. Reordering the guards is unsound (the\n    // ThrottlerGuard reads request.user.sub, which only THIS guard populates, so\n    // throttle-first would re-classify every authenticated SUPER_ADMIN as\n    // anonymous). Instead we account each auth FAILURE against a per-IP bucket\n    // (only failures increment it, so a valid operator's fan-out never counts)\n    // and emit a security event that reaches the incident pipeline.\n    @Inject(IpRateLimiterService)\n    private readonly failedAuthIpLimiter: IpRateLimiterService,\n    @Inject(SecurityEventService)\n    private readonly securityEvents: SecurityEventService,\n  ) {\n    // SECURITY (CRITICAL-001): JWT_SECRET length validation removed in WS2.B\n    // (2026-04-14). The check was a hold-over from the HS256 era — verifyAsync\n    // already uses getJwtVerifyOptions() which loads the RS256 public key and\n    // throws at startup if it is missing. There is no JWT_SECRET to validate\n    // anymore; reading it would also trip the ESLint no-restricted-syntax\n    // ban on JWT_SECRET reads (WS2.C).\n  }\n\n  async canActivate(context: ExecutionContext): Promise<boolean> {\n    // Hybrid-app boundary: this guard authenticates the HTTP surface only.\n    // admin-api-service also consumes NATS events (RPC context) — those are\n    // authenticated by the broker-verified mTLS client-cert CN (ADR-015) and\n    // the per-subject publish ACL (e.g. only farm-service may publish\n    // TenantOnboardingAck), NOT by a Bearer JWT, which does not exist on the\n    // NATS surface. Without this short-circuit the guard would call\n    // switchToHttp().getRequest() on an RPC context and reject every event.\n    if (context.getType() !== 'http') {\n      return true;\n    }\n\n    // Check if route is marked as public\n    const isPublic = this.reflector.getAllAndOverride<boolean>(IS_PUBLIC_KEY, [\n      context.getHandler(),\n      context.getClass(),\n    ]);\n\n    if (isPublic) {\n      return true;\n    }\n\n    const request = context.switchToHttp().getRequest<AuthenticatedRequest>();\n    const authHeader = request.headers.authorization;\n\n    if (!authHeader) {\n      return this.rejectAuth(\n        context,\n        request,\n        new UnauthorizedException('No authorization header provided'),\n      );\n    }\n\n    const [type, token] = authHeader.split(' ');\n\n    if (type !== 'Bearer' || !token) {\n      return this.rejectAuth(\n        context,\n        request,\n        new UnauthorizedException('Invalid authorization header format'),\n      );\n    }\n\n    try {\n      // BEFORE: jwt.verify() — synchronous, no algorithm restriction, no iss/aud validation.\n      // AFTER: JwtService.verifyAsync() with getJwtVerifyOptions() — async (non-blocking),\n      // RS256 algorithm enforced, issuer and audience validated at jsonwebtoken library level.\n      const payload = await this.jwtService.verifyAsync<JwtPayload>(\n        token,\n        getJwtVerifyOptions(this.configService),\n      );\n      enforceAccessTokenType(\n        payload,\n        this.logger,\n        this.configService.get<string>('NODE_ENV') === 'production',\n      );\n\n      // APA-367: mandatory post-verify revocation check. A signature-valid\n      // SUPER_ADMIN token whose session was force-logged-out, whose owner was\n      // deactivated/deleted, or whose password was reset must be rejected here —\n      // otherwise the token keeps working against admin-api until natural expiry,\n      // silently defeating the platform's emergency-cutoff controls. Consults\n      // both revocation namespaces (per-jti/user blacklist + user_blacklist epoch)\n      // via the shared primitive so admin-api cannot drift out of the contract\n      // that gateway-api and auth-service already enforce.\n      await enforceTokenNotRevoked(\n        payload,\n        {\n          tokenBlacklist: {\n            isValidToken: (jti: string): Promise<boolean> =>\n              this.tokenBlacklist.isBlacklisted(jti).then((blacklisted) => !blacklisted),\n          },\n          userTokenRevocation: this.userTokenRevocation,\n        },\n        this.logger,\n      );\n\n      // Normalize user roles - tekil role varsa array'e çevir\n      const userRoles = payload.roles || (payload.role ? [payload.role] : []);\n\n      // Attach user to request first (for later use in controllers).\n      // WHY both `sub` and `id`: the shared backend-common ThrottlerGuard (and\n      // every `@CurrentUser('sub')` consumer) reads the canonical JWT subject\n      // as `request.user.sub`. This guard historically exposed ONLY `id`, so\n      // the throttler saw `user.sub === undefined`, classified every\n      // authenticated SUPER_ADMIN as ANONYMOUS (20 req/60s) AND keyed the bucket\n      // by IP instead of user — a single operator's admin-panel fan-out tripped\n      // 429s. `id` stays as admin-api's local convention (controllers read it).\n      request.user = {\n        sub: payload.sub,\n        id: payload.sub,\n        email: payload.email,\n        roles: userRoles,\n        role: payload.role || userRoles[0],\n        tenantId: payload.tenantId,\n        // Read by AuditedOperationInterceptor for the mandatory mfaVerified column\n        // and by DestructiveActionGuard for the MFA freshness window (ADR-0011).\n        mfaVerified: payload.mfaVerified === true,\n        iat: payload.iat,\n        // ADR-0016: read by PlatformCapabilityGuard (route capability) and\n        // DestructiveActionGuard (break-glass). Narrowed to the closed enum so a\n        // forged or stale string can never act as a capability.\n        platformCapabilities: toPlatformCapabilities(payload.platformCapabilities),\n      };\n\n      const requestContext = requestContextStorage.getStore();\n      if (requestContext) {\n        requestContext.userId = payload.sub;\n        requestContext.tenantId = payload.tenantId;\n        // ADMIN-CRITICAL-102: the audit writer derives the actor from this\n        // frame; a request body can never name who acted.\n        requestContext.userEmail = payload.email;\n        requestContext.mfaVerified = payload.mfaVerified === true;\n      }\n\n      // Admin API is a platform-admin boundary. In the current auth model that\n      // platform actor is encoded as SUPER_ADMIN; decorators may narrow to that\n      // role, but must never widen admin-api access to tenant/module roles.\n      const decoratedRoles = this.reflector.getAllAndOverride<string[]>(ROLES_KEY, [\n        context.getHandler(),\n        context.getClass(),\n      ]);\n      const requiredRoles = (decoratedRoles || DEFAULT_ADMIN_ROLES).filter(\n        (role) => role.toUpperCase() === 'SUPER_ADMIN',\n      );\n      if (requiredRoles.length === 0) {\n        requiredRoles.push('SUPER_ADMIN');\n      }\n\n      // Case-insensitive role check\n      const hasRequiredRole = userRoles.some((userRole) =>\n        requiredRoles.some((required) => required.toUpperCase() === userRole.toUpperCase()),\n      );\n\n      if (!hasRequiredRole) {\n        // SECURITY: Log user ID only -- do not include email PII in logs (H-14)\n        this.logger.warn(\n          `Access denied for userId=${payload.sub}: ` +\n            `has roles [${userRoles.join(', ')}], requires one of [${requiredRoles.join(', ')}]`,\n        );\n        throw new ForbiddenException(`Access denied. Required roles: ${requiredRoles.join(', ')}`);\n      }\n\n      return true;\n    } catch (error) {\n      // A valid token with an insufficient role is NOT an authentication failure\n      // (it is a real, authenticated principal) — rethrow as-is; it must not fill\n      // the failed-auth IP bucket and is already logged at warn above.\n      if (error instanceof ForbiddenException) {\n        throw error;\n      }\n\n      // Rejections from enforceAccessTokenType / enforceTokenNotRevoked (wrong\n      // token type, missing jti, revoked token) — preserve their code by routing\n      // the original exception through the failed-auth accounting.\n      if (error instanceof UnauthorizedException) {\n        return this.rejectAuth(context, request, error);\n      }\n\n      if (error instanceof jwt.TokenExpiredError) {\n        return this.rejectAuth(context, request, new UnauthorizedException('Token has expired'));\n      }\n\n      if (error instanceof jwt.JsonWebTokenError) {\n        return this.rejectAuth(context, request, new UnauthorizedException('Invalid token'));\n      }\n\n      // Unexpected error — keep the stack trace at error level, then account the\n      // failure and reject.\n      this.logger.error(\n        `Authentication error for ${request.method} ${request.url}: ${(error as Error).message}`,\n        (error as Error).stack,\n      );\n      return this.rejectAuth(context, request, new UnauthorizedException('Authentication failed'));\n    }\n  }\n\n  /**\n   * Best-effort client IP: the left-most X-Forwarded-For hop (the original\n   * client through nginx), falling back to the request address. Keyed per-IP so\n   * a failed-auth flood from one source is bounded regardless of the (spoofable)\n   * identity a forged token might claim.\n   */\n  private getClientIp(request: AuthenticatedRequest): string {\n    const forwarded = headerValue(request.headers['x-forwarded-for']);\n    const firstHop = forwarded?.split(',')[0]?.trim();\n    return firstHop || request.ip || 'unknown';\n  }\n\n  /**\n   * APA-369: record a failed authentication against the per-IP bucket, emit the\n   * AUTH_TOKEN_REJECTED security event, and — once an IP crosses the failed-auth\n   * limit — emit RATE_LIMIT_EXCEEDED and reject with 429 (instead of 401) so\n   * token brute-force is APP-throttled (not merely nginx-throttled) and reaches\n   * the incident pipeline. Only auth FAILURES call this, so an authenticated\n   * operator's request fan-out from one IP never fills the bucket.\n   */\n  private async rejectAuth(\n    context: ExecutionContext,\n    request: AuthenticatedRequest,\n    unauthorized: UnauthorizedException,\n  ): Promise<never> {\n    const reason = reasonOf(unauthorized);\n    // WARN, not DEBUG: failed auth on the most privileged surface must be\n    // visible at production log levels.\n    this.logger.warn(`401 Unauthorized: ${reason} for ${request.method} ${request.url}`);\n\n    const ip = this.getClientIp(request);\n    const userAgent = headerValue(request.headers['user-agent']);\n    const limit = this.failedAuthIpLimiter.checkLimit(ip);\n\n    await this.securityEvents.publishTokenRejected({ reason, ip, userAgent });\n\n    if (!limit.allowed) {\n      const windowMs = this.configService.get<number>('IP_RATE_WINDOW_MS', 60000);\n      const maxPerWindow = this.configService.get<number>('IP_RATE_LIMIT', 100);\n      await this.securityEvents.publishRateLimitExceeded({\n        key: `admin-auth:ip:${ip}`,\n        limit: maxPerWindow,\n        windowMs,\n        count: maxPerWindow + 1,\n        ip,\n        userAgent,\n      });\n      const response = context\n        .switchToHttp()\n        .getResponse<{ setHeader(name: string, value: string): void }>();\n      if (limit.retryAfter) {\n        response.setHeader('Retry-After', String(limit.retryAfter));\n      }\n      throw new HttpException(\n        { code: 'TOO_MANY_FAILED_AUTH', message: 'Too many failed authentication attempts' },\n        HttpStatus.TOO_MANY_REQUESTS,\n      );\n    }\n\n    throw unauthorized;\n  }\n}\n","numLines":382,"startLine":1,"totalLines":382}}}

{"type":"user","message":{"role":"user","content":[{"tool_use_id":"toolu_01F9Gp14HgGmCN4vxRcuaCNL","type":"tool_result","content":"1\t/**\n2\t * Password Reset Controller - Security Tests\n3\t *\n4\t * Tests cover:\n5\t * - DTO validation (email, password)\n6\t * - Email enumeration prevention\n7\t * - Auth-service delegation for reset-token/password state\n8\t * - ThrottlePasswordReset decorator presence\n9\t * - Public decorator presence (bypass auth for these endpoints)\n10\t */\n11\timport { INestApplication, ValidationPipe, HttpStatus } from '@nestjs/common';\n12\timport { Test, TestingModule } from '@nestjs/testing';\n13\timport { AUTH_PUBLIC_COMMAND_SUBJECTS } from '@platform/event-contracts';\n14\timport { of, throwError } from 'rxjs';\n15\timport request from 'supertest';\n16\t\n17\timport { PasswordResetController } from '../password-reset.controller';\n18\t\n19\tdescribe('PasswordResetController Security', () => {\n20\t  let app: INestApplication;\n21\t\n22\t  type TestApp = Parameters<typeof request>[0];\n23\t  type PasswordResetResponseBody = {\n24\t    success?: boolean;\n25\t    message?: string;\n26\t  };\n27\t  type AuthNatsSend = jest.Mock<unknown, [string, unknown]>;\n28\t\n29\t  const mockAuthNatsClient = {\n30\t    send: jest.fn() as AuthNatsSend,\n31\t  };\n32\t\n33\t  function httpServer(): TestApp {\n34\t    return app.getHttpServer() as TestApp;\n35\t  }\n36\t\n37\t  function responseBody(res: request.Response): PasswordResetResponseBody {\n38\t    return res.body as PasswordResetResponseBody;\n39\t  }\n40\t\n41\t  function isRecord(value: unknown): value is Record<string, unknown> {\n42\t    return typeof value === 'object' && value !== null && !Array.isArray(value);\n43\t  }\n44\t\n45\t  function authCommandAt(index: number): Record<string, unknown> {\n46\t    const call = mockAuthNatsClient.send.mock.calls[index];\n47\t    if (!call) {\n48\t      throw new Error(`Expected auth NATS command at call index ${index}`);\n49\t    }\n50\t    const payload = call[1];\n51\t    if (!isRecord(payload)) {\n52\t      throw new Error(`Expected auth NATS command payload at call index ${index}`);\n53\t    }\n54\t    return payload;\n55\t  }\n56\t\n57\t  beforeAll(async () => {\n58\t    const module: TestingModule = await Test.createTestingModule({\n59\t      controllers: [PasswordResetController],\n60\t      providers: [\n61\t        { provide: 'AUTH_NATS_CLIENT', useValue: mockAuthNatsClient },\n62\t      ],\n63\t    }).compile();\n64\t\n65\t    app = module.createNestApplication();\n66\t    app.useGlobalPipes(\n67\t      new ValidationPipe({\n68\t        whitelist: true,\n69\t        forbidNonWhitelisted: true,\n70\t        transform: true,\n71\t      }),\n72\t    );\n73\t    await app.init();\n74\t  });\n75\t\n76\t  afterAll(async () => {\n77\t    await app.close();\n78\t  });\n79\t\n80\t  beforeEach(() => {\n81\t    jest.clearAllMocks();\n82\t    mockAuthNatsClient.send.mockReturnValue(of({ success: true }));\n83\t  });\n84\t\n85\t  // ========================================================================\n86\t  // 1. Forgot Password - Validation\n87\t  // ========================================================================\n88\t  describe('POST /auth/forgot-password - Validation', () => {\n89\t    it('should accept valid email', async () => {\n90\t      const res = await request(httpServer())\n91\t        .post('/auth/forgot-password')\n92\t        .send({ email: 'user@example.com' });\n93\t\n94\t      expect(res.status).toBe(HttpStatus.OK);\n95\t      expect(responseBody(res).success).toBe(true);\n96\t    });\n97\t\n98\t    it('should reject missing email', async () => {\n99\t      const res = await request(httpServer())\n100\t        .post('/auth/forgot-password')\n101\t        .send({});\n102\t\n103\t      expect(res.status).toBe(HttpStatus.BAD_REQUEST);\n104\t    });\n105\t\n106\t    it('should reject invalid email format', async () => {\n107\t      const res = await request(httpServer())\n108\t        .post('/auth/forgot-password')\n109\t        .send({ email: 'not-an-email' });\n110\t\n111\t      expect(res.status).toBe(HttpStatus.BAD_REQUEST);\n112\t    });\n113\t\n114\t    it('should reject extra fields (forbidNonWhitelisted)', async () => {\n115\t      const res = await request(httpServer())\n116\t        .post('/auth/forgot-password')\n117\t        .send({ email: 'user@example.com', maliciousField: 'hacked' });\n118\t\n119\t      expect(res.status).toBe(HttpStatus.BAD_REQUEST);\n120\t    });\n121\t  });\n122\t\n123\t  // ========================================================================\n124\t  // 2.
