* Password Reset Controller - Security Tests\n *\n * Tests cover:\n * - DTO validation (email, password)\n * - Email enumeration prevention\n * - Auth-service delegation for reset-token/password state\n * - ThrottlePasswordReset decorator presence\n * - Public decorator presence (bypass auth for these endpoints)\n */\nimport { INestApplication, ValidationPipe, HttpStatus } from '@nestjs/common';\nimport { Test, TestingModule } from '@nestjs/testing';\nimport { AUTH_PUBLIC_COMMAND_SUBJECTS } from '@platform/event-contracts';\nimport { of, throwError } from 'rxjs';\nimport request from 'supertest';\n\nimport { PasswordResetController } from '../password-reset.controller';\n\ndescribe('PasswordResetController Security', () => {\n  let app: INestApplication;\n\n  type TestApp = Parameters<typeof request>[0];\n  type PasswordResetResponseBody = {\n    success?: boolean;\n    message?: string;\n  };\n  type AuthNatsSend = jest.Mock<unknown, [string, unknown]>;\n\n  const mockAuthNatsClient = {\n    send: jest.fn() as AuthNatsSend,\n  };\n\n  function httpServer(): TestApp {\n    return app.getHttpServer() as TestApp;\n  }\n\n  function responseBody(res: request.Response): PasswordResetResponseBody {\n    return res.body as PasswordResetResponseBody;\n  }\n\n  function isRecord(value: unknown): value is Record<string, unknown> {\n    return typeof value === 'object' && value !== null && !Array.isArray(value);\n  }\n\n  function authCommandAt(index: number): Record<string, unknown> {\n    const call = mockAuthNatsClient.send.mock.calls[index];\n    if (!call) {\n      throw new Error(`Expected auth NATS command at call index ${index}`);\n    }\n    const payload = call[1];\n    if (!isRecord(payload)) {\n      throw new Error(`Expected auth NATS command payload at call index ${index}`);\n    }\n    return payload;\n  }\n\n  beforeAll(async () => {\n    const module: TestingModule = await Test.createTestingModule({\n      controllers: [PasswordResetController],\n      providers: [\n        { provide: 'AUTH_NATS_CLIENT', useValue: mockAuthNatsClient },\n      ],\n    }).compile();\n\n    app = module.createNestApplication();\n    app.useGlobalPipes(\n      new ValidationPipe({\n        whitelist: true,\n        forbidNonWhitelisted: true,\n        transform: true,\n      }),\n    );\n    await app.init();\n  });\n\n  afterAll(async () => {\n    await app.close();\n  });\n\n  beforeEach(() => {\n    jest.clearAllMocks();\n    mockAuthNatsClient.send.mockReturnValue(of({ success: true }));\n  });\n\n  // ========================================================================\n  // 1. Forgot Password - Validation\n  // ========================================================================\n  describe('POST /auth/forgot-password - Validation', () => {\n    it('should accept valid email', async () => {\n      const res = await request(httpServer())\n        .post('/auth/forgot-password')\n        .send({ email: 'user@example.com' });\n\n      expect(res.status).toBe(HttpStatus.OK);\n      expect(responseBody(res).success).toBe(true);\n    });\n\n    it('should reject missing email', async () => {\n      const res = await request(httpServer())\n        .post('/auth/forgot-password')\n        .send({});\n\n      expect(res.status).toBe(HttpStatus.BAD_REQUEST);\n    });\n\n    it('should reject invalid email format', async () => {\n      const res = await request(httpServer())\n        .post('/auth/forgot-password')\n        .send({ email: 'not-an-email' });\n\n      expect(res.status).toBe(HttpStatus.BAD_REQUEST);\n    });\n\n    it('should reject extra fields (forbidNonWhitelisted)', async () => {\n      const res = await request(httpServer())\n        .post('/auth/forgot-password')\n        .send({ email: 'user@example.com', maliciousField: 'hacked' });\n\n      expect(res.status).toBe(HttpStatus.BAD_REQUEST);\n    });\n  });\n\n  // ========================================================================\n  // 2. Email Enumeration Prevention\n  // ========================================================================\n  describe('Email enumeration prevention', () => {\n    it('should return success even for non-existent email', async () => {\n      const res = await request(httpServer())\n        .post('/auth/forgot-password')\n        .send({ email: 'nonexistent@example.com' });\n\n      expect(res.status).toBe(HttpStatus.OK);\n      expect(responseBody(res).success).toBe(true);\n      expect(responseBody(res).message).toContain('If an account');\n    });\n\n    it('should return same response shape for existing email', async () => {\n      const res = await request(httpServer())\n        .post('/auth/forgot-password')\n        .send({ email: 'exists@example.com' });\n\n      expect(res.status).toBe(HttpStatus.OK);\n      expect(responseBody(res).success).toBe(true);\n      expect(responseBody(res).message).toContain('If an account');\n    });\n\n    it('should return success even on internal error', async () => {\n      mockAuthNatsClient.send.mockReturnValueOnce(throwError(() => new Error('NATS connection failed')));\n\n      const res = await request(httpServer())\n        .post('/auth/forgot-password')\n        .send({ email: 'user@example.com' });\n\n      expect(res.status).toBe(HttpStatus.OK);\n      expect(responseBody(res).success).toBe(true);\n    });\n  });\n\n  // ========================================================================\n  // 3. Token Security\n  // ========================================================================\n  describe('Token security', () => {\n    it('should delegate forgot password to auth-service without local token storage', async () => {\n      await request(httpServer())\n        .post('/auth/forgot-password')\n        .send({ email: 'Test@Test.COM' });\n\n      expect(mockAuthNatsClient.send).toHaveBeenCalledWith(\n        AUTH_PUBLIC_COMMAND_SUBJECTS.REQUEST_PASSWORD_RESET,\n        expect.objectContaining({ email: 'test@test.com' }),\n      );\n    });\n\n    it('should not send raw reset URL material from admin-api', async () => {\n      await request(httpServer())\n        .post('/auth/forgot-password')\n        .send({ email: 'test@test.com' });\n\n      const command = authCommandAt(0);\n\n      expect(command).not.toHaveProperty('rawToken');\n      expect(command).not.toHaveProperty('resetLink');\n      expect(command).not.toHaveProperty('tokenHash');\n    });\n  });\n\n  // ========================================================================\n  // 4. Reset Password - Validation\n  // ========================================================================\n  describe('POST /auth/reset-password - Validation', () => {\n    it('should reject missing token', async () => {\n      const res = await request(httpServer())\n        .post('/auth/reset-password')\n        .send({ newPassword: 'ValidPass123!' });\n\n      expect(res.status).toBe(HttpStatus.BAD_REQUEST);\n    });\n\n    it('should reject missing password', async () => {\n      const res = await request(httpServer())\n        .post('/auth/reset-password')\n        .send({ token: 'some-token' });\n\n      expect(res.status).toBe(HttpStatus.BAD_REQUEST);\n    });\n\n    it('should reject password shorter than 8 characters', async () => {\n      const res = await request(httpServer())\n        .post('/auth/reset-password')\n        .send({ token: 'some-token', newPassword: 'short' });\n\n      expect(res.status).toBe(HttpStatus.BAD_REQUEST);\n    });\n\n    it('should reject password longer than 128 characters', async () => {\n      const longPassword = 'a'.repeat(129);\n      const res = await request(httpServer())\n        .post('/auth/reset-password')\n        .send({ token: 'some-token', newPassword: longPassword });\n\n      expect(res.status).toBe(HttpStatus.BAD_REQUEST);\n    });\n\n    it('should accept valid token and password', async () => {\n      const res = await request(httpServer())\n        .post('/auth/reset-password')\n        .send({ token: 'valid-token-string', newPassword: 'ValidPass123!' });\n\n      expect(res.status).toBe(HttpStatus.OK);\n      expect(responseBody(res).success).toBe(true);\n    });\n\n    it('should reject extra fields', async () => {\n      const res = await request(httpServer())\n        .post('/auth/reset-password')\n        .send({ token: 'tok', newPassword: 'ValidPass123!', role: 'SUPER_ADMIN' });\n\n      expect(res.status).toBe(HttpStatus.BAD_REQUEST);\n    });\n  });\n\n  // ========================================================================\n  // 5. Reset Password - Token Verification\n  // ========================================================================\n  describe('Reset password token verification', () => {\n    it('should reject invalid/expired token', async () => {\n      mockAuthNatsClient.send.mockReturnValueOnce(of({\n        success: false,\n        errorCode: 'INVALID_OR_EXPIRED_TOKEN',\n      }));\n\n      const res = await request(httpServer())\n        .post('/auth/reset-password')\n        .send({ token: 'invalid-token', newPassword: 'NewPass123!' });\n\n      expect(res.status).toBe(HttpStatus.BAD_REQUEST);\n      expect(responseBody(res).message).toContain('Invalid or expired');\n    });\n\n    it('should delegate the new password to auth-service for hashing and persistence', async () => {\n      await request(httpServer())\n        .post('/auth/reset-password')\n        .send({ token: 'valid-token', newPassword: 'NewSecurePass123!' });\n\n      expect(mockAuthNatsClient.send).toHaveBeenCalledWith(\n        AUTH_PUBLIC_COMMAND_SUBJECTS.RESET_PASSWORD,\n        expect.objectContaining({\n          token: 'valid-token',\n          newPassword: 'NewSecurePass123!',\n        }),\n      );\n    });\n\n    it('should not issue local auth table update statements', async () => {\n      await request(httpServer())\n        .post('/auth/reset-password')\n        .send({ token: 'valid-token', newPassword: 'NewSecurePass123!' });\n\n      expect(mockAuthNatsClient.send).toHaveBeenCalledTimes(1);\n    });\n  });\n\n  // ========================================================================\n  // 6. SQL Injection Prevention\n  // ========================================================================\n  describe('SQL injection prevention', () => {\n    it('should reject invalid email before auth-service delegation', async () => {\n      await request(httpServer())\n        .post('/auth/forgot-password')\n        .send({ email: \"test@test.com'; DROP TABLE users;--\" });\n\n      expect(mockAuthNatsClient.send).not.toHaveBeenCalled();\n    });\n  });\n});\n","numLines":296,"startLine":1,"totalLines":296}}}

What this spec keeps true:\n6\t *\n7\t *   1. auth-service's TokenService refuses a SUPER_ADMIN token for an\n8\t *      un-enrolled account once the switch has passed, and names the account\n9\t *      in a security event before then — asserted as unit behaviour in the\n10\t *      service's own spec, whose test titles are pinned here so the behaviour\n11\t *      cannot be deleted with its test.\n12\t *   2. Every irreversible admin handler — a DELETE, or a handler named\n13\t *      erase / archive / purge / drop / release / export / terminate /\n14\t *      revoke / rollback / deprovision — carries @Destructive(), which\n15\t *      installs DestructiveActionGuard itself. Public (unauthenticated)\n16\t *      handlers are exempt: a password-reset by token has no principal.\n17\t *   3. The switch is the only switch: no committed compose or env file sets\n18\t *      MFA_REQUIRED_FOR_CROSS_TENANT=false, and the auth-service compose\n19\t *      entry states SUPER_ADMIN_MFA_ENFORCED_AT as a literal that parses.\n20\t *   4. `security.mfa_enabled` has no reader anywhere: an operator toggle for\n21\t *      a mandatory control is gone.\n22\t */\n23\t\n24\timport { execFileSync } from 'node:child_process';\n25\timport { readFileSync } from 'node:fs';\n26\timport { resolve } from 'node:path';\n27\t\n28\timport * as yaml from 'js-yaml';\n29\t\n30\timport { parsePlatformAdminMfaPolicy } from '../../libs/backend-common/src/security/platform-admin-mfa-policy';\n31\t\n32\timport { allAdminMutationHandlers } from 'utf8');\n38\t}\n39\t\n40\tfunction stripComments(src: string): string {\n41\t  return src\n42\t    .replace(/\\/\\*[\\s\\S]*?\\*\\//g, '')\n43\t    .split('\\n')\n44\t    .map((line) => line.replace(/(?<!:)\\/\\/.*$/, ''))\n45\t    .join('\\n');\n46\t}\n47\t\n48\tfunction gitFiles(pathspecs: string[]): string[] {\n49\t  return execFileSync(\n50\t    'git',\n51\t    ['-C', REPO_ROOT, 'ls-files', '--cached', '--others', '--exclude-standard', '--', ...pathspecs],\n52\t    {\n53\t      encoding: 'utf8',\n54\t    },\n55\t  )\n56\t    .split('\\n')\n57\t    .filter(Boolean);\n58\t}\n59\t\n60\tfunction gitGrepFiles(args: string[]): string[] {\n61\t  try {\n62\t    return execFileSync('git', ['-C', REPO_ROOT, 'grep', '-l', '--untracked', ...args], {\n63\t      encoding: 'utf8',\n64\t    })\n65\t      .split('\\n')\n66\t      .filter(Boolean);\n67\t  } catch (err: unknown) {\n68\t    if ((err as { status?: number }).status === 1) return [];\n69\t    throw err;\n70\t  }\n71\t}\n72\t\n73\tconst IRREVERSIBLE_NAME =\n74\t  /(erase|archive|purge|drop|release|export|terminate|revoke|rollback|deprovision)/i;\n75\t\n76\tdescribe('INVARIANT (ADR-0011): platform-admin MFA — one switch, one mechanism', () => {\n77\t  it('auth-service refuses or records an un-enrolled SUPER_ADMIN at token issue, and its spec proves both', () => {\n78\t    const service = stripComments(\n79\t      read('apps/auth-service/src/modules/authentication/services/token.service.ts'),\n80\t    );\n81\t    expect(service).toMatch(/user\\.role === Role\\.SUPER_ADMIN && !user\\.mfaEnabled/);\n82\t    expect(service).toMatch(/readPlatformAdminMfaPolicy\\(\\)/);\n83\t    expect(service).toMatch(/if \\(policy\\.enforced\\) \\{\\s*throw new ForbiddenException/);\n84\t un-enrolled account once enforcement has started',\n89\t    );\n90\t    expect(spec).toContain(\n91\t      'records an un-enrolled SUPER_ADMIN in detective mode and still mints the token',\n92\t    );\n93\t  });\n94\t\n95\t  it('every irreversible admin handler carries @Destructive(), which installs its own guard', () => {\n96\t    const decorator = stripComments(\n97\t      read('libs/backend-common/src/decorators/destructive.decorator.ts'),\n98\t    );\n99\t    expect(decorator).toMatch(/UseGuards\\(DestructiveActionGuard\\)/);\n100\t    const unguarded: string[] = [];\n101\t    for (const handler of allAdminMutationHandlers()) {\n102\t      const irreversible = handler.verb === 'Delete' || IRREVERSIBLE_NAME.test(handler.name);\n103\t      if (!irreversible || handler.isPublic) continue;\n104\t      if (!/@Destructive\\(/.test(handler.block)) unguarded.push(handler.id);\n105\t    }\n106\t    expect(unguarded).toEqual([]);\n107\t  });\n108\t\n109\t  it('the destructive-event sink is bound globally in admin-api so detective mode leaves a row', () => {\n110\t    const auditModule = expect(auditModule).toMatch(/@Global\\(\\)/);\n113\t    expect(auditModule).toMatch(/exports: \\[[^\\]]*DESTRUCTIVE_EVENT_SINK/);\n114\t  });\n115\t\n116\t  it('no committed compose or env file switches cross-tenant MFA off', () => {\n117\t    const offenders = gitGrepFiles([\n118\t      '-E',\n119\t      '-e',\n120\t * INVARIANT — platform-admin MFA has one switch and one mechanism\n * (ADR-0011, SEC-CRITICAL-058).\n *\n * The mechanism ships now; enforcement is dated. What this spec keeps true:\n *\n *   1. auth-service's TokenService refuses a SUPER_ADMIN token for an\n *      un-enrolled account once the switch has passed, and names the account\n *      in a security event before then — asserted as unit behaviour in the\n *      service's own spec, whose test titles are pinned here so the behaviour\n *      cannot be deleted with its test.\n *   2. Every irreversible admin handler — a DELETE, or a handler named\n *      erase / archive / purge / drop / release / export / terminate /\n *      revoke / rollback / deprovision — carries @Destructive(), which\n *      installs DestructiveActionGuard itself. Public (unauthenticated)\n *      handlers are exempt: a password-reset by token has no principal.\n *   3. The switch is the only switch: no committed compose or env file sets\n *      MFA_REQUIRED_FOR_CROSS_TENANT=false, and the auth-service compose\n *      entry states SUPER_ADMIN_MFA_ENFORCED_AT as a literal that parses.\n *   4. `security.mfa_enabled` has no reader anywhere: an operator toggle for\n *      a mandatory control is gone.\n */\n\nimport { execFileSync } from 'node:child_process';\nimport { readFileSync } from 'node:fs';\nimport { resolve } from 'node:path';\n\nimport * as yaml from 'js-yaml';\n\nimport { parsePlatformAdminMfaPolicy } from '../../libs/backend-common/src/security/platform-admin-mfa-policy';\n\nimport { allAdminMutationHandlers } from './lib/admin-mutation-handlers';\n\nconst REPO_ROOT = resolve(__dirname, '..', '..');\n\nfunction read(rel: string): string {\n  return readFileSync(resolve(REPO_ROOT, rel), 'utf8');\n}\n\nfunction stripComments(src: string): string {\n  return src\n    .replace(/\\/\\*[\\s\\S]*?\\*\\//g, '')\n    .split('\\n')\n    .map((line) => line.replace(/(?<!:)\\/\\/.*$/, ''))\n    .join('\\n');\n}\n\nfunction gitFiles(pathspecs: string[]): string[] {\n  return execFileSync(\n    'git',\n    ['-C', REPO_ROOT, 'ls-files', '--cached', '--others', '--exclude-standard', '--', ...pathspecs],\n    {\n      encoding: 'utf8',\n    },\n  )\n    .split('\\n')\n    .filter(Boolean);\n}\n\nfunction gitGrepFiles(args: string[]): string[] {\n  try {\n    return execFileSync('git', ['-C', REPO_ROOT, 'grep', '-l', '--untracked', ...args], {\n      encoding: 'utf8',\n    })\n      .split('\\n')\n      .filter(Boolean);\n  } catch (err: unknown) {\n    if ((err as { status?: number }).status === 1) return [];\n    throw err;\n  }\n}\n\nconst IRREVERSIBLE_NAME =\n  /(erase|archive|purge|drop|release|export|terminate|revoke|rollback|deprovision)/i;\n\ndescribe('INVARIANT (ADR-0011): platform-admin MFA — one switch, one mechanism', () => {\n  it('auth-service refuses or records an un-enrolled SUPER_ADMIN at token issue, and its spec proves both', () => {\n    const service = stripComments(\n      read('apps/auth-service/src/modules/authentication/services/token.service.ts'),\n    );\n    expect(service).toMatch(/user\\.role === Role\\.SUPER_ADMIN && !user\\.mfaEnabled/);\n    expect(service).toMatch(/readPlatformAdminMfaPolicy\\(\\)/);\n    expect(service).toMatch(/if \\(policy\\.enforced\\) \\{\\s*throw new ForbiddenException/);\n    const spec = read(\n      'apps/auth-service/src/modules/authentication/services/token.service.spec.ts',\n    );\n    expect(spec).toContain(\n      'refuses a SUPER_ADMIN token for an un-enrolled account once enforcement has started',\n    );\n    expect(spec).toContain(\n      'records an un-enrolled SUPER_ADMIN in detective mode and still mints the token',\n    );\n  });\n\n  it('every irreversible admin handler carries @Destructive(), which installs its own guard', () => {\n    const decorator = stripComments(\n      read('libs/backend-common/src/decorators/destructive.decorator.ts'),\n    );\n    expect(decorator).toMatch(/UseGuards\\(DestructiveActionGuard\\)/);\n    const unguarded: string[] = [];\n    for (const handler of allAdminMutationHandlers()) {\n      const irreversible = handler.verb === 'Delete' || IRREVERSIBLE_NAME.test(handler.name);\n      if (!irreversible || handler.isPublic) continue;\n      if (!/@Destructive\\(/.test(handler.block)) unguarded.push(handler.id);\n    }\n    expect(unguarded).toEqual([]);\n  });\n\n  it('the destructive-event sink is bound globally in admin-api so detective mode leaves a row', () => {\n    const auditModule = stripComments(read('apps/admin-api-service/src/audit/audit.module.ts'));\n    expect(auditModule).toMatch(/provide: DESTRUCTIVE_EVENT_SINK/);\n    expect(auditModule).toMatch(/@Global\\(\\)/);\n    expect(auditModule).toMatch(/exports: \\[[^\\]]*DESTRUCTIVE_EVENT_SINK/);\n  });\n\n  it('no committed compose or env file switches cross-tenant MFA off', () => {\n    const offenders = gitGrepFiles([\n      '-E',\n      '-e',\n      \"MFA_REQUIRED_FOR_CROSS_TENANT[=:]\\\\s*'?false'?\",","numLines":120,"startLine":1,"totalLines":155}}}

expect(module).toContain('AuthPublicNatsHandler');\n  });\n});\n\ndescribe('INVARIANT: auth module catalog writes are auth-service owned', () => {\n  it('delegates admin-api module catalog mutations {"type":"user","message":{"role":"user","content":[{"tool_use_id":"toolu_01DcK59hAwk4bsUJwMgpLAwv","type":"tool_result","content":"30\t * Public REST endpoints for device provisioning (no auth required)\n31\t *\n32\t * SECURITY: Rate limiting is applied to prevent brute-force attacks\n33\t * - Install script: 5 requests per minute per IP\n34\t * - Device activation: 3 requests per minute per IP\n35\t *\n36\t * These endpoints are called by:\n37\t * 1. The installer script (GET /install/:deviceCode)\n38\t * 2. The edge agent (POST /api/devices/activate)\n39\t *\n40\t * @SkipTenantGuard bypasses the global TenantGuard since these endpoints\n41\t * are anonymous (no tenant context). Each endpoint has its own validation.\n42\t */\n43\t@Controller()\n44\t@Public()\n45\t@UseGuards(SimpleRateLimitGuard)\n46\t@SkipTenantGuard()\n47\texport class ProvisioningController {\n48\t  private readonly logger = new Logger(ProvisioningController.name);\n49\t"}]},"parent_tool_use_id":null,"session_id":"b73213a0-f835-4f7c-b793-eff657206976","uuid":"5b1f2681-199b-4195-91ac-50346bd1d4f9","timestamp":"2026-09-19T13:48:07.455Z","tool_use_result":{"type":"text","file":{"filePath":"/home/gharunner/actions-runner/_work/aquaculture_platform/aquaculture_platform/aria-worktrees/req-AIR-aria-evidence-judge-725e0f69acd6/apps/sensor-service/src/edge-device/provisioning.controller.ts","content":" * Public REST endpoints for device provisioning (no auth required)\n *\n * SECURITY: Rate limiting is applied to prevent brute-force attacks\n * - Install script: 5 requests per minute per IP\n * - Device activation: 3 requests per minute per IP\n *\n * These endpoints are called by:\n * 1. The edge agent (POST /api/devices/activate)\n *\n * @SkipTenantGuard bypasses the global TenantGuard since these endpoints\n * are anonymous (no tenant context). {"type":"user","message":{"role":"user","content":[{"tool_use_id":"toolu_0174QCb6fNTeQbLR7WehTgXH","type":"tool_result","content":"30\t# hang observed during V8 verification.\n31\t#\n32\t# The replacement below matches the canonical evidence-ref languages\n33\t# (path | path:line | path:line:content) with no overlapping\n34\t# quantifiers. Worst-case time stays linear in input length even for\n35\t# pathological inputs that the pre-V8.3 ReDoS pattern hung on.\n36\t#\n37\t# Plan ARIA-V8.6 — accept the third `path:line:content` form. The\n38\t# `plan_synthesizer` (V7.9) emits evidence_refs as\n39\t# `path:line:<excerpt>` triplets so operators can spot-check the\n40\t# claimed line text without opening every file. The pre-V8.6 regex\n41\t# rejected those refs as malformed because it only allowed the\n42\t# 2-part `path:line` form, so the agent's response evidence_refs\n43\t# (which echo the request's refs) hit `agent_evidence_ref_malformed`\n44\t# at the kernel validator. The `(?::.*)?` clause captures the\n45\t# trailing `:<excerpt>` non-greedily without backtracking risk\n46\t# (atomic alternation; the path group is anchored by `[^\\s:]+`).\n47\t_AGENT_REF_RE = re.compile(r\"^(?P<path>[^\\s:]+)(?::(?P<line>\\d+)(?::.*)?)?$\")\n48\t\n49\t\n50\t# An unverified ref is rejected under a code that says WHOSE gap it is. Three\n51\t# grades reach this mapping and only one of them is about the evidence:\n52\t#\n53\t# * ``worktree_candidate`` — the tree was read and disagrees: the agent's.\n54\t# * ``baseline_unavailable`` — the caller threaded no target_sha, so no\n55\t#   comparison was attempted (2026-08-09): the harness's.\n56\t# * ``verification_unavailable`` — a comparison was attempted and git did not\n57\t#   answer inside its bound (2026-09-12, loaded host): the harness's.\n58\t#\n59\t# One table for both evidence sources so the tool-output side and the agent\n60\t# side cannot drift apart again — the agent side kept a single code for a\n61\t# month after the tool side had split.\n62\t_UNVERIFIED_GRADE_CODE_SUFFIX: dict[str, str] = {\n63\t    \"baseline_unavailable\": \"evidence_baseline_unavailable\",\n64\t    \"verification_unavailable\": \"evidence_verification_unavailable\",\n65\t}\n66\t_UNVERIFIED_DEFAULT_CODE_SUFFIX = \"evidence_not_repo_verified\"\n67\t\n68\t# The rejection codes that mean \"the validator could not verify\", on either\n69\t# source. The executor releases a submission rejected ONLY for these under a\n70\t# harness-class reason (`evidence_verification_unavailable`) so the request\n71\t# is retried without burning its requeue budget; every other rejection stays\n72\t# the request's fault. Kept next to the mapping that mints them.\n73\tEVIDENCE_VERIFICATION_UNAVAILABLE_CODES: frozenset[str] = frozenset({\n74\t    f\"{source}_{_UNVERIFIED_GRADE_CODE_SUFFIX['verification_unavailable']}\"\n75\t    for source in (\"tool_output\", \"agent\")\n76\t})\n77\t\n78\t\n79\tdef _unverified_evidence_code(source: str, grade: Any) -> str:\n80\t    \"\"\"``<source>_evidence_<why>`` for a ref that is not ``repo_verified``.\"\"\"\n81\t    suffix = _UNVERIFIED_GRADE_CODE_SUFFIX.get(str(grade), _UNVERIFIED_DEFAULT_CODE_SUFFIX)\n82\t    return f\"{source}_{suffix}\"\n83\t\n84\t\n85\t# The agent-side \"could not verify\" code, minted by the same table as the\n86\t# per-ref grades so a decision that could not even resolve its evidence\n87\t# target (`agent_invocations._verified_evidence_target_sha`) rejects under\n88\t# a code the executor's release seam already classifies as the harness's.\n89\tAGENT_EVIDENCE_VERIFICATION_UNAVAILABLE_CODE = _unverified_evidence_code("}]},"parent_tool_use_id":null,"session_id":"b73213a0-f835-4f7c-b793-eff657206976","uuid":"e08be687-c5c5-4329-9443-c25ff5766483","timestamp":"2026-09-19T13:48:46.526Z","tool_use_result":{"type":"text","file":{"filePath":"/home/gharunner/actions-runner/_work/aquaculture_platform/aquaculture_platform/aria-worktrees/req-AIR-aria-evidence-judge-725e0f69acd6/aria-kernel/aria_kernel/evidence_validator.py","content":"# hang observed during V8 verification.\n#\n# The replacement below matches the canonical evidence-ref languages\n# (path | path:line | path:line:content) with no overlapping\n# quantifiers. Worst-case time stays linear in input length even for\n# pathological inputs that the pre-V8.3 ReDoS pattern hung on.\n#\n# Plan ARIA-V8.6 — accept the third `path:line:content` form. The\n# `plan_synthesizer` (V7.9) emits evidence_refs as\n# `path:line:<excerpt>` triplets so operators can spot-check the\n# claimed line text without opening every file. The pre-V8.6 regex\n# rejected those refs as malformed because it only allowed the\n# 2-part `path:line` form, so the agent's response evidence_refs\n# (which echo the request's refs) hit `agent_evidence_ref_malformed`\n# at the kernel validator. The `(?::.*)?` clause captures the\n# trailing `:<excerpt>` non-greedily without backtracking risk\n# (atomic alternation; the path group is anchored by `[^\\s:]+`).\n_AGENT_REF_RE = re.compile(r\"^(?P<path>[^\\s:]+)(?::(?P<line>\\d+)(?::.*)?)?$\")\n\n\n# An unverified ref is rejected under a code that says WHOSE gap it is. Three\n# grades reach this mapping and only one of them is about the evidence:\n#\n# * ``worktree_candidate`` — the tree was read and disagrees: the agent's.\n# * ``baseline_unavailable`` — the caller threaded no target_sha, so no\n#   comparison was attempted (2026-08-09): the harness's.\n# * ``verification_unavailable`` — a comparison was attempted and git did not\n#   answer inside its bound (2026-09-12, loaded host): the harness's.\n#\n# One table for both evidence sources so the tool-output side and the agent\n# side cannot drift apart again — the agent side kept a single code for a\n# month after the tool side had split.\n_UNVERIFIED_GRADE_CODE_SUFFIX: dict[str, str] = {\n    \"baseline_unavailable\": \"evidence_baseline_unavailable\",\n    \"verification_unavailable\": \"evidence_verification_unavailable\",\n}\n_UNVERIFIED_DEFAULT_CODE_SUFFIX = \"evidence_not_repo_verified\"\n\n# The rejection codes that mean \"the validator could not verify\", on either\n# source. The executor releases a submission rejected ONLY for these under a\n# harness-class reason (`evidence_verification_unavailable`) so the request\n# is retried without burning its requeue budget; every other rejection stays\n# the request's fault. Kept next to the mapping that mints them.\nEVIDENCE_VERIFICATION_UNAVAILABLE_CODES: frozenset[str] = frozenset({\n    f\"{source}_{_UNVERIFIED_GRADE_CODE_SUFFIX['verification_unavailable']}\"\n    for source in (\"tool_output\", \"agent\")\n})\n\n\ndef _unverified_evidence_code(source: str, grade: Any) -> str:\n    \"\"\"``<source>_evidence_<why>`` for a ref that is not ``repo_verified``.\"\"\"\n    suffix = _UNVERIFIED_GRADE_CODE_SUFFIX.get(str(grade), _UNVERIFIED_DEFAULT_CODE_SUFFIX)\n    return f\"{source}_{suffix}\"\n\n\n# The agent-side \"could not verify\" code, minted by the same table as the\n# per-ref grades so a decision that could not even resolve its evidence\n# target (`agent_invocations._verified_evidence_target_sha`) rejects under\n# a code the executor's release seam already classifies as the harness's.\nAGENT_EVIDENCE_VERIFICATION_UNAVAILABLE_CODE = _unverified_evidence_code(","numLines":60,"startLine":30,"totalLines":774}}}

{"type":"user","message":{"role":"user","content":[{"tool_use_id":"toolu_01ThxuYNrZCM4XwCYb5wJQqW","type":"tool_result","content":"460\tdef _check_agent_ref(\n461\t    ref: str,\n462\t    *,\n463\t    root: Path,\n464\t    errors: list[dict[str, Any]],\n465\t    checked: list[str],\n466\t    allow_self_output: bool = False,\n467\t    allow_kernel_artifacts: bool = False,\n468\t) -> None:\n469\t    # Y7 follow-through (ORPHAN-708) — the kernel's OWN adjudication mint\n470\t    # (human_required_adjudication.open_adjudication) issues\n471\t    # ``human-required:<request-id>`` refs, and the first real panel drain\n472\t    # showed this validator rejecting them: mint-side and law-side of the\n473\t    # same kernel disagreed, every panel opinion died submit_rejected, and\n474\t    # the rejection BURNED the envelope's requeue budget. The ref is a\n475\t    # LEDGER POINTER, not a repo path — the load-bearing verification\n476\t    # happens at fold time (fold_adjudication / adjudicate_human_required\n477\t    # resolve the record from the store before any disposition acts), so\n478\t    # the law admits the pointer as checked rather than pretending a repo\n479\t    # check that cannot exist for state-store records.\n480\t    if _is_ledger_pointer_ref(ref):\n481\t        checked.append(ref)\n482\t        return\n483\t    if allow_kernel_artifacts:\n484\t        admissible, reason = _kernel_artifact_verdict(ref, root)\n485\t        if admissible:\n486\t            checked.append(ref)\n487\t            return\n488\t        if reason != \"artifact_not_recorded_in_results_ledger\":\n489\t            # It IS an artifact path — just not one the ledger vouches for.\n490\t            # Naming the failure is the point: a silent fall-through to the\n491\t            # repo check would report \"not repo verified\" about a file that\n492\t            # was never meant to be in the repo.\n493\t            errors.append({\n494\t                \"code\": \"agent_evidence_artifact_unverifiable\",\n495\t                \"ref\": ref,\n496\t                \"reason\": reason,\n497\t            })\n498\t            return\n499\t    parsed = _parse_agent_ref(ref)\n500\t    if parsed is None:\n501\t        errors.append({\"code\": \"agent_evidence_ref_malformed\", \"ref\": ref})\n502\t        return\n503\t    path, line = parsed\n504\t    # Plan 024 v3 §H-5 — canonical-resolve BEFORE the SELF_OUTPUT\n505\t    # prefix check. Pre-fix the prefix match operated on the\n506\t    # lexically-normalized string; a `src/../aria-tools/...` traversal\n507\t    # could bypass detection if normalize_path didn't fully collapse\n508\t    # it. Post-fix the resolution runs first, then SELF_OUTPUT is\n509\t    # decided on the canonical posix-relative form.\n510\t    from .tool_registry import GovernanceError as _GE\n511\t    try:\n512\t        rel_str, absolute = _canonical_evidence_path(path, root)\n513\t    except _GE as exc:\n514\t        msg = str(exc)\n515\t        if msg.startswith(\"evidence_path_outside_repo\"):\n516\t            errors.append({\"code\": \"agent_evidence_path_escapes_workspace\", \"path\": path})\n517\t        else:\n518\t            errors.append({\"code\": \"agent_evidence_path_unresolvable\", \"path\": path,\n519\t                           \"error\": msg})\n520\t        return\n521\t    # ARIA self-output / runtime artefacts are NOT admissible evidence\n522\t    # for an agent claim (CLAUDE.md L1, Plan 016 §Agent output untrusted).\n523\t    if not allow_self_output and rel_str.startswith(SELF_OUTPUT_MARKERS):\n524\t        errors.append({\"code\": \"agent_evidence_self_output\", \"path\": rel_str})\n525\t        return\n526\t    if not absolute.exists() or not absolute.is_file():\n527\t        errors.append({\"code\": \"agent_evidence_path_missing\", \"path\": path})\n528\t        return\n529\t    checked.append(rel_str)\n530\t    if line is None:\n531\t        return\n532\t    if line <= 0:\n533\t        errors.append({\"code\": \"agent_evidence_line_invalid\", \"path\": path, \"line\": line})\n534\t        return\n535\t    line_count = len(absolute.read_text(encoding=\"utf-8\", errors=\"replace\").splitlines())\n536\t    if line > line_count:\n537\t        errors.append({\"code\": \"agent_evidence_line_missing\", \"path\": path, \"line\": line, \"line_count\": line_count})\n538\t\n539\t\n540\tdef validate_agent_response_evidence(\n541\t    *,\n542\t    response: dict[str, Any],\n543\t    workspace_root: str | Path,\n544\t    request: dict[str, Any] | None = None,\n545\t    probe_session: GitProbeSession | None = None,\n546\t) -> dict[str, Any]:\n547\t    \"\"\"Re-fetch every evidence ref claimed by an agent response and verify it exists in the repo.\n548\t\n549\t    Why: Plan 016 V-24 — agent output is data, not truth. The contract\n550\t    layer (agent_contract.validate_response) checks the SHAPE of the\n551\t    satisfaction matrix and the response envelope. This function checks\n552\t    that every evidence_ref the agent attached to its verdicts actually\n553\t    points at a real file (and line, when given) at the workspace SHA.\n554\t    A response that passes both gates is contractually safe to publish\n555\t    as a CONVERGED plan / promoted finding.\n556\t\n557\t    Returns: {\n558\t        \"valid\": bool,\n559\t        \"errors\": [{\"code\": ..., \"path\": ..., ...}],\n560\t        \"checked_refs\": [<sorted unique paths>],\n561\t    }\n562\t    \"\"\"\n563\t    root = Path(workspace_root).resolve()\n564\t    errors: list[dict[str, Any]] = []\n565\t    checked: list[str] = []\n566\t    evidence_envelopes: list[dict[str, Any]] = []\n567\t    # ORPHAN-CRITICAL-734 — role-scoped, not global. Only a role whose\n568\t    # SUBJECT is another agent's verdict may cite that verdict's artifact,\n569\t    # and only when the results ledger still vouches for its bytes."}]},"parent_tool_use_id":null,"session_id":"b73213a0-f835-4f7c-b793-eff657206976","uuid":"6aa7bdc0-40bc-448c-8565-7b79234fbc25","timestamp":"2026-09-19T13:48:47.674Z","tool_use_result":{"type":"text","file":{"filePath":"/home/gharunner/actions-runner/_work/aquaculture_platform/aquaculture_platform/aria-worktrees/req-AIR-aria-evidence-judge-725e0f69acd6/aria-kernel/aria_kernel/evidence_validator.py","content":"def _check_agent_ref(\n    ref: str,\n    *,\n    root: Path,\n    errors: list[dict[str, Any]],\n    checked: list[str],\n    allow_self_output: bool = False,\n    allow_kernel_artifacts: bool = False,\n) -> None:\n    # Y7 follow-through (ORPHAN-708) — the kernel's OWN adjudication mint\n    # (human_required_adjudication.open_adjudication) issues\n    # ``human-required:<request-id>`` refs, and the first real panel drain\n    # showed this validator rejecting them: mint-side and law-side of the\n    # same kernel disagreed, every panel opinion died submit_rejected, and\n    # the rejection BURNED the envelope's requeue budget. The ref is a\n    # LEDGER POINTER, not a repo path — the load-bearing verification\n    # happens at fold time (fold_adjudication / adjudicate_human_required\n    # resolve the record from the store before any disposition acts), so\n    # the law admits the pointer as checked rather than pretending a repo\n    # check that cannot exist for state-store records.\n    if _is_ledger_pointer_ref(ref):\n        checked.append(ref)\n        return\n    if allow_kernel_artifacts:\n        admissible, reason = _kernel_artifact_verdict(ref, root)\n        if admissible:\n            checked.append(ref)\n            return\n        if reason != \"artifact_not_recorded_in_results_ledger\":\n            # It IS an artifact path — just not one the ledger vouches for.\n            # Naming the failure is the point: a silent fall-through to the\n            # repo check would report \"not repo verified\" about a file that\n            # was never meant to be in the repo.\n            errors.append({\n                \"code\": \"agent_evidence_artifact_unverifiable\",\n                \"ref\": ref,\n                \"reason\": reason,\n            })\n            return\n    parsed = _parse_agent_ref(ref)\n    if parsed is None:\n        errors.append({\"code\": \"agent_evidence_ref_malformed\", \"ref\": ref})\n        return\n    path, line = parsed\n    # Plan 024 v3 §H-5 — canonical-resolve BEFORE the SELF_OUTPUT\n    # prefix check. Pre-fix the prefix match operated on the\n    # lexically-normalized string; a `src/../aria-tools/...` traversal\n    # could bypass detection if normalize_path didn't fully collapse\n    # it. Post-fix the resolution runs first, then SELF_OUTPUT is\n    # decided on the canonical posix-relative form.\n    from .tool_registry import GovernanceError as _GE\n    try:\n        rel_str, absolute = _canonical_evidence_path(path, root)\n    except _GE as exc:\n        msg = str(exc)\n        if msg.startswith(\"evidence_path_outside_repo\"):\n            errors.append({\"code\": \"agent_evidence_path_escapes_workspace\", \"path\": path})\n        else:\n            errors.append({\"code\": \"agent_evidence_path_unresolvable\", \"path\": path,\n                           \"error\": msg})\n        return\n    # ARIA self-output / runtime artefacts are NOT admissible evidence\n    # for an agent claim (CLAUDE.md L1, Plan 016 §Agent output untrusted).\n    if not allow_self_output and rel_str.startswith(SELF_OUTPUT_MARKERS):\n        errors.append({\"code\": \"agent_evidence_self_output\", \"path\": rel_str})\n        return\n    if not absolute.exists() or not absolute.is_file():\n        errors.append({\"code\": \"agent_evidence_path_missing\", \"path\": path})\n        return\n    checked.append(rel_str)\n    if line is None:\n        return\n    if line <= 0:\n        errors.append({\"code\": \"agent_evidence_line_invalid\", \"path\": path, \"line\": line})\n        return\n    line_count = len(absolute.read_text(encoding=\"utf-8\", errors=\"replace\").splitlines())\n    if line > line_count:\n        errors.append({\"code\": \"agent_evidence_line_missing\", \"path\": path, \"line\": line, \"line_count\": line_count})\n\n\ndef validate_agent_response_evidence(\n    *,\n    response: dict[str, Any],\n    workspace_root: str | Path,\n    request: dict[str, Any] | None = None,\n    probe_session: GitProbeSession | None = None,\n) -> dict[str, Any]:\n    \"\"\"Re-fetch every evidence ref claimed by an agent response and verify it exists in the repo.\n\n    Why: Plan 016 V-24 — agent output is data, not truth. The contract\n    layer (agent_contract.validate_response) checks the SHAPE of the\n    satisfaction matrix and the response envelope. This function checks\n    that every evidence_ref the agent attached to its verdicts actually\n    points at a real file (and line, when given) at the workspace SHA.\n    A response that passes both gates is contractually safe to publish\n    as a CONVERGED plan / promoted finding.\n\n    Returns: {\n        \"valid\": bool,\n        \"errors\": [{\"code\": ..., \"path\": ..., ...}],\n        \"checked_refs\": [<sorted unique paths>],\n    }\n    \"\"\"\n    root = Path(workspace_root).resolve()\n    errors: list[dict[str, Any]] = []\n    checked: list[str] = []\n    evidence_envelopes: list[dict[str, Any]] = []\n    # ORPHAN-CRITICAL-734 — role-scoped, not global. Only a role whose\n    # SUBJECT is another agent's verdict may cite that verdict's artifact,\n    # and only when the results ledger still vouches for its bytes.","numLines":110,"startLine":460,"totalLines":774}}}

ONE probe session for the whole submission (evidence_probe): the\n574\t    # baseline is resolved once for all refs, and every git probe of this\n575\t    # decision shares one liveness clock — the seam that used to hand each\n576\t    # and every ref grades `verification_unavailable` rather than being\n580\t    # compared against an anchor the submitter did not cite.\n581\t    probe_session = probe_session if probe_session is not None else GitProbeSession()\n582\t\n583\t    response_refs = response.get(\"evidence_refs\") or []\n584\t    if not isinstance(response_refs, list):\n585\t        errors.append({\"code\": \"agent_evidence_refs_not_list\"})\n586\t        response_refs = []\n587\t    for ref in response_refs:\n588\t        if not isinstance(ref, str) or not ref.strip():\n589\t            errors.append({\"code\": \"agent_evidence_ref_not_string\"})\n590\t            continue\n591\t        _check_agent_ref(\n592\t            ref, root=root, errors=errors, checked=checked,\n593\t            allow_kernel_artifacts=allow_kernel_artifacts,\n594\t        )\n595\t        if _is_ledger_pointer_ref(ref):\n596\t            continue  # repo classification below cannot describe it (the artifact\n600\t            # is state-store-owned and was never meant to be committed).\n601\t            continue\n602\t        envelope = classify_evidence_ref(\n603\t            ref,\n604\t            workspace_root=root,\n605\t            source_hint=\"agent_output\",\n606\t            target_sha=_request_target_sha(request),\n607\t            probe_session=probe_session,\n608\t        )\n609\t        evidence_envelopes.append(envelope.to_dict())\n610\t        try:\n611\t            EvidencePolicy.require_repo_verified(envelope)\n612\t        except GovernanceError as exc:\n613\t            errors.append({\n614\t                \"code\": _unverified_evidence_code(\"agent\", envelope.trust_grade),\n615\t                \"ref\": ref,\n616\t                \"reason\": str(exc),\n617\t            })\n618\t\n619\t    # Plan 024 §B-2 — satisfaction_matrix non-empty enforcement.\n620\t    # Pre-fix an agent response with `satisfaction_matrix: []` passed\n621\t    # consensus because the matrix-iteration loop simply never ran.\n622\t    # {"type":"user","message":{"role":"user","content":[{"tool_use_id":"toolu_01GRwVEF8KGC3YfGgkP5GKAS","type":"tool_result","content":"2230\t    # WHY: claude -p stream-json emits JSONL events\n2231\t    # ({\"result\": \"...\", \"total_cost_usd\": ..., ...}) where the agent's final message may contain the\n2232\t    # actual aria/agent-response/v1 envelope as fenced ```json``` block\n2233\t    # or as final JSON block. Writing raw JSONL to output_path means\n2234\t    # kernel submit-result reads runtime telemetry instead of the envelope,\n2235\t    # which fails:\n2236\t    #   response_schema: missing required fields:\n2237\t    #     ['$schema', 'request_id', 'claim_id', 'agent_id', 'role',\n2238\t    #      'status', 'satisfaction_matrix']\n2239\t    #\n2240\t    # HOW: parse JSONL, extract the final message, find the embedded\n2241\t    # envelope JSON, INJECT mandatory identity fields ($schema,\n2242\t    # request_id, claim_id, agent_id, role, status) from the known\n2243\t    # ci_executor context (these aren't the agent's job — the agent\n2244\t    # only knows its plan content), synthesize a satisfaction_matrix\n2245\t    # from must_satisfy with verdict=satisfied when the agent omitted\n2246\t    # it, then write the corrected envelope to output_path.\n2247\t    #\n2248\t    # Tier hierarchy: Tier-2 (Make it automatic) — the envelope shape\n2249\t    # is now produced correctly by default; agents don't have to know\n2250\t    # internal kernel identity fields.\n2251\t    if completed.stdout:\n2252\t        envelope = _build_envelope_from_claude_output(\n2253\t            raw_stdout=completed.stdout,\n2254\t            request_id=request_id,\n2255\t            claim_id=claim_id or \"\",\n2256\t            agent_id=agent_id or \"\",\n2257\t            role=role,\n2258\t            subagent_type=subagent_type,\n2259\t            must_satisfy=must_satisfy or [],\n2260\t            dispatch_model=agent_profile.model,\n2261\t        )\n2262\t        envelope[\"details\"][\"agent_contract_hash\"] = agent_contract.contract_hash\n2263\t        output_path.parent.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)\n2264\t        _write_sanitized_envelope(output_path, envelope)\n2265\t        resolved_transcript_path.parent.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)\n2266\t        resolved_transcript_path.write_text(completed.stdout, encoding=\"utf-8\")\n2267\t        # Plan ARIA-V3.1-D3 — per-LLM-call cost attribution. Gated on\n2268\t        # _MOCK_MODE_AT_ENTRY frozen sentinel (V3.1-D2) so a mid-run\n2269\t        # CLAUDE_CLI_MOCK flip cannot rewrite mock-mode classification"}]},"parent_tool_use_id":null,"session_id":"b73213a0-f835-4f7c-b793-eff657206976","uuid":"2ccdfa26-ace7-48f0-bf7b-380aab919a58","timestamp":"2026-09-19T13:49:33.245Z","tool_use_result":{"type":"text","file":{"filePath":"/home/gharunner/actions-runner/_work/aquaculture_platform/aquaculture_platform/aria-worktrees/req-AIR-aria-evidence-judge-725e0f69acd6/tools/aria-poc/ci_executor.py","content":"    # WHY: claude -p stream-json emits JSONL events\n    # ({\"result\": \"...\", \"total_cost_usd\": ..., ...}) where the agent's final message may contain the\n    # actual aria/agent-response/v1 envelope as fenced ```json``` block\n    # or as final JSON block. Writing raw JSONL to output_path means\n    # kernel submit-result reads runtime telemetry instead of the envelope,\n    # which fails:\n    #   response_schema: missing required fields:\n    #     ['$schema', 'request_id', 'claim_id', 'agent_id', 'role',\n    #      'status', 'satisfaction_matrix']\n    #\n    # HOW: parse JSONL, extract the final message, find the embedded\n    # envelope JSON, INJECT mandatory identity fields ($schema,\n    # request_id, claim_id, agent_id, role, status) from the known\n    # ci_executor context (these aren't the agent's job — the agent\n    # only knows its plan content), synthesize a satisfaction_matrix\n    # from must_satisfy with verdict=satisfied when the agent omitted\n    # it, then write the corrected envelope to output_path.\n    #\n    # Tier hierarchy: Tier-2 (Make it automatic) — the envelope shape\n    # is now produced correctly by default; agents don't have to know\n    # internal kernel identity fields.\n    if completed.stdout:\n        envelope = _build_envelope_from_claude_output(\n            raw_stdout=completed.stdout,\n            request_id=request_id,\n            claim_id=claim_id or \"\",\n            agent_id=agent_id or \"\",\n            role=role,\n            subagent_type=subagent_type,\n            must_satisfy=must_satisfy or [],\n            dispatch_model=agent_profile.model,\n        )\n        envelope[\"details\"][\"agent_contract_hash\"] = agent_contract.contract_hash\n        output_path.parent.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)\n        _write_sanitized_envelope(output_path, envelope)\n        resolved_transcript_path.parent.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)\n        resolved_transcript_path.write_text(completed.stdout, encoding=\"utf-8\")\n        # Plan ARIA-V3.1-D3 — per-LLM-call cost attribution. Gated on\n        # _MOCK_MODE_AT_ENTRY frozen sentinel (V3.1-D2) so a mid-run\n        # CLAUDE_CLI_MOCK flip cannot rewrite mock-mode classification","numLines":40,"startLine":2230,"totalLines":5265}}}

{"type":"user","message":{"role":"user","content":[{"tool_use_id":"toolu_01Bm87gvhPURRaBFqHQyHod1","type":"tool_result","content":"2496:def _build_envelope_from_claude_output(\n2497-    *,\n2498-    raw_stdout: str,\n2499-    request_id: str,\n2500-    claim_id: str,\n2501-    agent_id: str,\n2502-    role: str,\n2503-    subagent_type: str,\n2504-    must_satisfy: list[dict[str, Any]],\n2505-    dispatch_model: str | None = None,\n2506-) -> dict[str, Any]:\n2507-    \"\"\"Convert ``claude -p stream-json`` JSONL into a kernel-valid envelope.\n2508-\n2509-    Claude Code emits JSONL events. ARIA keeps those raw events out of\n2510-    artifacts, extracts the final agent message, and injects the\n2511-    lease-bound identity fields that the agent cannot know.\n2512-    \"\"\"\n2513-    events = parse_claude_jsonl(raw_stdout)\n2514-    agent_text = extract_final_message(events)\n2515-    if not agent_text:\n2516-        agent_text = raw_stdout\n2517-    extracted = _extract_envelope_json(agent_text) or {}\n2518-\n2519-    envelope: dict[str, Any] = {\n2520-        \"$schema\": \"aria/agent-response/v1\",\n2521-        \"request_id\": request_id,\n2522-        \"claim_id\": claim_id,\n2523-        \"agent_id\": agent_id,\n2524-        \"role\": role,\n2525-        \"status\": str(extracted.get(\"status\") or \"submitted\"),\n2526-    }\n2527-\n2528-    matrix_in = extracted.get(\"satisfaction_matrix\")\n2529-    if isinstance(matrix_in, list) and matrix_in:\n2530-        envelope[\"satisfaction_matrix\"] = matrix_in\n2531-    else:\n2532-        # Synthesize from must_satisfy so the kernel's non-empty-matrix\n2533-        # check passes — as UNVERIFIED rows (ARIA-AUDIT-024). {"type":"user","message":{"role":"user","content":[{"tool_use_id":"toolu_014xxBgESGukaA3HakPU5Z7F","type":"tool_result","content":"742:def generate_ai_consensus(\n743-    *,\n744-    tool_id: str,\n745-    cycle_id: str | None = None,\n746-    min_confidence: float = CONSENSUS_MIN_CONFIDENCE,\n747-    workspace_root: str | Path | None = None,\n748-    base_dir: str | Path | None = None,\n749-    judge_weights: dict[str, float] | None = None,\n750-    conformal_floor: float | None = None,\n751-) -> dict[str, Any]:\n752-    \"\"\"Kalibre Zekâ Z2a/Z2c — both knobs default OFF, preserving the\n753-    legacy gate bit for bit:\n754-\n755-    * ``judge_weights`` (judge_id -> Beta-posterior precision mean, from\n756-      ``calibrated_intelligence.judge_weights_from_calibration``): the\n757-      unanimity requirement becomes a WEIGHTED vote — the winning verdict\n758-      must carry a strict majority of total weight AND every dissenter's\n759-      weight share stays under the margin; with two equal judges a single\n760-      dissenter still escalates, exactly like unanimity, so behaviour only\n761-      shifts when the fleet grows or posteriors genuinely separate.\n762-    * ``conformal_floor`` (from ``conformal_threshold`` over past CORRECT\n763-      consensus confidences): a passing consensus whose confidence falls\n764-      below the floor abstains to a human (``conformal_abstain``) with the\n765-      distribution-free guarantee that at most ~alpha of genuinely-correct\n766-      consensuses are escalated.\n767-    \"\"\"\n768-    if min_confidence < 0 or min_confidence > 1:\n769-        raise GovernanceError(\"min_confidence must be between 0 and 1\")\n770-    ai_rows = [\n771-        row\n772-        for row in load_feedback(tool_id=tool_id, base_dir=base_dir)\n773-        if row.get(\"source_type\") == \"ai_judge\" and (cycle_id is None or _feedback_cycle(row, base_dir) == cycle_id)\n774-    ]\n775-    # JJ-1 - the dedup key remembers HOW MANY judges VOTED on the settled row.\n776-    # A group settled at 2 judges must be re-settleable once the anchor\n777-    # arbiter answers, otherwise the 3rd judge's verdict is minted, paid for,\n778-    # and then discarded by an idempotency guard - the anchor class would be\n779-    # unreachable by construction. Attendance (not agreement) is the right\n780-    # key: a third judge who DISAGREES leaves the agreement count at 2, and\n781-    # keying on agreement would re-settle that group every cycle forever.\n782-    # Re-running with no new judge still appends nothing, and the count is\n783-    # bounded by the number of distinct judges.\n784-    existing_consensus: dict[tuple[str, str, str], int] = {}\n785-    for row in load_feedback(tool_id=tool_id, base_dir=base_dir):\n786-        if row.get(\"source_type\") != \"ai_consensus\":\n787-            continue\n788-        key = _judgment_key(row)\n789-        existing_consensus[key] = max(\n790-            existing_consensus.get(key, 0), consensus_judges_voted(row),\n791-        )\n792-    grouped: dict[tuple[str, str, str], dict[str, dict[str, Any]]] = {}\n793-    for row in ai_rows:\n794-        run_id = str(row.get(\"run_id\") or \"\")\n795-        finding_id = str(row.get(\"finding_id\") or \"\")\n796-        group_id = str(row.get(\"judgment_group_id\") or \"\")\n797-        judge_id = str(row.get(\"judge_id\") or \"\")\n798-        if not run_id or not finding_id or not judge_id:\n799-            continue\n800-        grouped.setdefault((run_id, finding_id, group_id), {})[judge_id] = row\n801-\n802-    consensus_rows = []\n803-    uncertainties = []\n804-    for key, by_judge in sorted(grouped.items()):\n805-        rows = list(by_judge.values())\n806-        run_id, finding_id, group_id = key\n807-        if key in existing_consensus and existing_consensus[key] >= len(rows):\n808-            continue\n809-        if len(rows) < 2:\n810-            uncertainties.append(_consensus_uncertainty(tool_id, run_id, finding_id, group_id, \"single_judge\"))\n811-            continue\n812-        verdicts = {str(row.get(\"verdict\") or \"\") for row in rows}\n813-        if judge_weights is None:\n814-            if len(verdicts) != 1:\n815-                uncertainties.append(_consensus_uncertainty(tool_id, run_id, finding_id, group_id, \"judge_disagreement\"))\n816-                continue\n817-        else:\n818-            # Z2a — weighted vote. Weight-by-verdict; unknown judges weigh\n819-            # at the neutral prior mean so a new judge neither dominates\n820-            # nor vanishes. Strict-majority + margin: the winner needs\n821-            # > 0.5 + margin of total weight, which with two equal judges\n822-            # degenerates to unanimity — the legacy guarantee survives."}]},"parent_tool_use_id":null,"session_id":"b73213a0-f835-4f7c-b793-eff657206976","uuid":"9d3c5b00-6642-499b-9d2a-a4382157a21b","timestamp":"2026-09-19T13:49:57.207Z","tool_use_result":{"mode":"content","numFiles":0,"filenames":[],"content":"742:def generate_ai_consensus(\n743-    *,\n744-    tool_id: str,\n745-    cycle_id: str | None = None,\n746-    min_confidence: float = CONSENSUS_MIN_CONFIDENCE,\n747-    workspace_root: str | Path | None = None,\n748-    base_dir: str | Path | None = None,\n749-    judge_weights: dict[str, float] | None = None,\n750-    conformal_floor: float | None = None,\n751-) -> dict[str, Any]:\n752-    \"\"\"Kalibre Zekâ Z2a/Z2c — both knobs default OFF, preserving the\n753-    legacy gate bit for bit:\n754-\n755-    * ``judge_weights`` (judge_id -> Beta-posterior precision mean, from\n756-      ``calibrated_intelligence.judge_weights_from_calibration``): the\n757-      unanimity requirement becomes a WEIGHTED vote — the winning verdict\n758-      must carry a strict majority of total weight AND every dissenter's\n759-      weight share stays under the margin; {"type":"user","message":{"role":"user","content":[{"tool_use_id":"toolu_01Dfj3DUt1xE9z7K1nvKaqqg","type":"tool_result","content":"200\t                group_id=str(request.get(\"judgment_group_id\") or arb_block.get(\"group_id\") or \"\"),\n201\t                reason=str(arb_reason),\n202\t                cycle_id=request.get(\"cycle_id\"),\n203\t                base_dir=base_dir,\n204\t            )\n205\t            return {\"kind\": \"consensus_uncertainty\", **row}\n206\t    verdict_block = _verdict_field(details)\n207\t\n208\t    tool_id = request.get(\"tool_id\") or verdict_block.get(\"tool_id\")\n209\t    run_id = request.get(\"run_id\") or verdict_block.get(\"run_id\")\n210\t    finding_id = request.get(\"finding_id\") or verdict_block.get(\"finding_id\")\n211\t    if not (tool_id and run_id and finding_id):\n212\t        raise GovernanceError(\n213\t            \"judge bridge requires tool_id, run_id, finding_id in request or response.details.verdict; \"\n214\t            f\"got tool_id={tool_id!r}, run_id={run_id!r}, finding_id={finding_id!r}\"\n215\t        )\n216\t\n217\t    verdict = verdict_block.get(\"verdict\")\n218\t    if verdict not in FEEDBACK_VERDICTS:\n219\t        raise GovernanceError(\n220\t            f\"judge bridge expected verdict in {FEEDBACK_VERDICTS}, got {verdict!r}\"\n221\t        )\n222\t    severity = _coerce_severity(verdict_block.get(\"severity\") or details.get(\"severity\"))\n223\t    # D1 (Kapalı Döngü) — judge identity. `response[\"agent_id\"]` is the\n224\t    # EXECUTOR's identity (`ci-executor:gha-<run>`), not the judge's: reading\n225\t    # it collapsed every judge drained by one workflow run into a single\n226\t    # voter (consensus structurally impossible) while judges drained by\n227\t    # DIFFERENT runs counted as distinct voters with meaningless identity.\n228\t    # The judge's real identity is the subagent the executor invoked\n229\t    # (`details.agent_subagent_type`, force-stamped by ci_executor); the\n230\t    # mock lane and legacy envelopes carry it as `verdict.judge_id`.\n231\t    judge_id = details.get(\"agent_subagent_type\") or verdict_block.get(\"judge_id\")\n232\t    if not judge_id:\n233\t        raise GovernanceError(\n234\t            \"judge bridge requires details.agent_subagent_type \"\n235\t            \"(or details.verdict.judge_id)\"\n236\t        )\n237\t    if str(judge_id).startswith(\"ci-executor:\"):\n238\t        # Refuse loudly instead of silently repairing: an executor-shaped\n239\t        # identity means the producer regressed, and a repaired row would\n240\t        # hide that while still poisoning per-judge calibration.\n241\t        raise GovernanceError(\n242\t            f\"judge bridge refuses executor-shaped judge identity: {judge_id!r}\"\n243\t        )\n244\t\n245\t    # The unit-interval gate, not a bare isinstance: True is an int and\n246\t    # float(\"nan\") is a float to isinstance, and both would ride a judge\n247\t    # verdict into calibration as if they were probabilities.\n248\t    confidence = confidence_in_unit_interval(verdict_block.get(\"confidence\"))\n249\t\n250\t    note = (\n251\t        verdict_block.get(\"rationale\")\n252\t        or verdict_block.get(\"note\")\n253\t        or response.get(\"rationale\")\n254\t        or \"envelope-routed judge submission\"\n255\t    )\n256\t    if not isinstance(note, str) or not note.strip():\n257\t        note = \"envelope-routed judge submission\"\n258\t\n259\t    raw_evidence_refs = verdict_block.get(\"evidence_refs\") or response.get(\"evidence_refs\") or []\n260\t    evidence_refs = [str(r) for r in raw_evidence_refs if isinstance(r, str) and r.strip()]\n261\t\n262\t    row = record_operator_feedback(\n263\t        tool_id=str(tool_id),\n264\t        run_id=str(run_id),\n265\t        finding_id=str(finding_id),\n266\t        verdict=verdict,\n267\t        severity=severity,\n268\t        note=note,\n269\t        source_type=\"ai_judge\",\n270\t        judge_id=str(judge_id),\n271\t        # ORPHAN-HIGH-781 — mint-first doctrine, same as judgment_group_id\n272\t        # and finding_fingerprint above: the model the DISPATCHER resolved\n273\t        # (`details.agent_dispatch_model`, force-stamped by ci_executor from\n274\t        # the runtime profile) outranks the judge's own verdict.model\n275\t        # self-report. The anchor distinct-model count must not trust a\n276\t        # string the judged agent wrote about itself; the self-report\n277\t        # survives only as the fallback for mock/legacy envelopes that\n278\t        # predate the stamp.\n279\t        model=details.get(\"agent_dispatch_model\") or verdict_block.get(\"model\"),\n280\t        prompt_hash=verdict_block.get(\"prompt_hash\"),\n281\t        confidence=float(confidence) if confidence is not None else None,\n282\t        rationale=str(note),\n283\t        evidence_refs=evidence_refs,\n284\t        # D1 — group identity comes from the MINT (judge_fanout's canonical\n285\t        # `judge:<tool>:<run>:<finding>`), never from the judge's own payload:\n286\t        # a judge echoing its request id as group id split one finding's two\n287\t        # verdicts across two groups-of-one, so the real disagreement on\n288\t        # anthropic.provider.ts:3 never met itself and never escalated.\n289\t        judgment_group_id=request.get(\"judgment_group_id\") or verdict_block.get(\"judgment_group_id\"),\n290\t        # ORPHAN-HIGH-765 — the fingerprint follows the SAME D1 doctrine as\n291\t        # the group id above: mint first, envelope only as fallback. On live\n292\t        # state 7 of 8 ai_judge rows carried an empty fingerprint because\n293\t        # the mint never carried it and no prompt asks the judge to echo it,\n294\t        # so every consensus row settled from those rows inherited the empty\n295\t        # key — and promote_consensus_findings silently skipped it forever.\n296\t        finding_fingerprint=request.get(\"finding_fingerprint\") or verdict_block.get(\"finding_fingerprint\"),\n297\t        base_dir=base_dir,\n298\t    )\n299\t    return row\n300\t\n301\t\n302\tdef persist_supporting_payload(\n303\t    *,\n304\t    request: dict[str, Any],"}]},"parent_tool_use_id":null,"session_id":"b73213a0-f835-4f7c-b793-eff657206976","uuid":"cde3cf58-b09a-41c4-aab7-accd5ccb3691","timestamp":"2026-09-19T13:50:07.687Z","tool_use_result":{"type":"text","file":{"filePath":"/home/gharunner/actions-runner/_work/aquaculture_platform/aquaculture_platform/aria-worktrees/req-AIR-aria-evidence-judge-725e0f69acd6/aria-kernel/aria_kernel/judgment_bridge.py","content":"                group_id=str(request.get(\"judgment_group_id\") or arb_block.get(\"group_id\") or \"\"),\n                reason=str(arb_reason),\n                cycle_id=request.get(\"cycle_id\"),\n                base_dir=base_dir,\n            )\n            return {\"kind\": \"consensus_uncertainty\", **row}\n    verdict_block = _verdict_field(details)\n\n    tool_id = request.get(\"tool_id\") or verdict_block.get(\"tool_id\")\n    run_id = request.get(\"run_id\") or verdict_block.get(\"run_id\")\n    finding_id = request.get(\"finding_id\") or verdict_block.get(\"finding_id\")\n    if not (tool_id and run_id and finding_id):\n        raise GovernanceError(\n            \"judge bridge requires tool_id, run_id, finding_id in request or response.details.verdict; \"\n            f\"got tool_id={tool_id!r}, run_id={run_id!r}, finding_id={finding_id!r}\"\n        )\n\n    verdict = verdict_block.get(\"verdict\")\n    if verdict not in FEEDBACK_VERDICTS:\n        raise GovernanceError(\n            f\"judge bridge expected verdict in {FEEDBACK_VERDICTS}, got {verdict!r}\"\n        )\n    severity = _coerce_severity(verdict_block.get(\"severity\") or details.get(\"severity\"))\n    # D1 (Kapalı Döngü) — judge identity. `response[\"agent_id\"]` is the\n    # EXECUTOR's identity (`ci-executor:gha-<run>`), not the judge's: reading\n    # it collapsed every judge drained by one workflow run into a single\n    # voter (consensus structurally impossible) while judges drained by\n    # DIFFERENT runs counted as distinct voters with meaningless identity.\n    # The judge's real identity is the subagent the executor invoked\n    # (`details.agent_subagent_type`, force-stamped by ci_executor); the\n    # mock lane and legacy envelopes carry it as `verdict.judge_id`.\n    judge_id = details.get(\"agent_subagent_type\") or verdict_block.get(\"judge_id\")\n    if not judge_id:\n        raise GovernanceError(\n            \"judge bridge requires details.agent_subagent_type \"\n            \"(or details.verdict.judge_id)\"\n        )\n    if str(judge_id).startswith(\"ci-executor:\"):\n        # Refuse loudly instead of silently repairing: an executor-shaped\n        # identity means the producer regressed, and a repaired row would\n        # hide that while still poisoning per-judge calibration.\n        raise GovernanceError(\n            f\"judge bridge refuses executor-shaped judge identity: {judge_id!r}\"\n        )\n\n    # The unit-interval gate, not a bare isinstance: True is an int and\n    # float(\"nan\") is a float to isinstance, and both would ride a judge\n    # verdict into calibration as if they were probabilities.\n    confidence = confidence_in_unit_interval(verdict_block.get(\"confidence\"))\n\n    note = (\n        verdict_block.get(\"rationale\")\n        or verdict_block.get(\"note\")\n        or response.get(\"rationale\")\n        or \"envelope-routed judge submission\"\n    )\n    if not isinstance(note, str) or not note.strip():\n        note = \"envelope-routed judge submission\"\n\n    raw_evidence_refs = verdict_block.get(\"evidence_refs\") or response.get(\"evidence_refs\") or []\n    evidence_refs = [str(r) for r in raw_evidence_refs if isinstance(r, str) and r.strip()]\n\n    row = record_operator_feedback(\n        tool_id=str(tool_id),\n        run_id=str(run_id),\n        finding_id=str(finding_id),\n        verdict=verdict,\n        severity=severity,\n        note=note,\n        source_type=\"ai_judge\",\n        judge_id=str(judge_id),\n        # ORPHAN-HIGH-781 — mint-first doctrine, same as judgment_group_id\n        # and finding_fingerprint above: the model the DISPATCHER resolved\n        # (`details.agent_dispatch_model`, force-stamped by ci_executor from\n        # the runtime profile) outranks the judge's own verdict.model\n        # self-report. The anchor distinct-model count must not trust a\n        # string the judged agent wrote about itself; the self-report\n        # survives only as the fallback for mock/legacy envelopes that\n        # predate the stamp.\n        model=details.get(\"agent_dispatch_model\") or verdict_block.get(\"model\"),\n        prompt_hash=verdict_block.get(\"prompt_hash\"),\n        confidence=float(confidence) if confidence is not None else None,\n        rationale=str(note),\n        evidence_refs=evidence_refs,\n        # D1 — group identity comes from the MINT (judge_fanout's canonical\n        # `judge:<tool>:<run>:<finding>`), never from the judge's own payload:\n        # a judge echoing its request id as group id split one finding's two\n        # verdicts across two groups-of-one, so the real disagreement on\n        # anthropic.provider.ts:3 never met itself and never escalated.\n        judgment_group_id=request.get(\"judgment_group_id\") or verdict_block.get(\"judgment_group_id\"),\n        # ORPHAN-HIGH-765 — the fingerprint follows the SAME D1 doctrine as\n        # the group id above: mint first, envelope only as fallback. On live\n        # state 7 of 8 ai_judge rows carried an empty fingerprint because\n        # the mint never carried it and no prompt asks the judge to echo it,\n        # so every consensus row settled from those rows inherited the empty\n        # key — and promote_consensus_findings silently skipped it forever.\n        finding_fingerprint=request.get(\"finding_fingerprint\") or verdict_block.get(\"finding_fingerprint\"),\n        base_dir=base_dir,\n    )\n    return row\n\n\ndef persist_supporting_payload(\n    *,\n    request: dict[str, Any],","numLines":105,"startLine":200,"totalLines":388}}}

\"public-write-endpoint-without-allowlist:apps/admin-api-service/src/auth/password-reset.controller.ts:98\",\n      \"verdict\": \"false_positive\",\n      \"severity\": \"high\",\n      \"judge_id\": \"aria-evidence-judge\",\n      \"model\": \"claude-opus-5\",\n      \"prompt_hash\": null,\n      \"confidence\": 0.86,\n      \"rationale\": \"The adapter's structural detection is accurate: the resetPassword declaration starts at line 98 (the @AuditedOperation decorator), carries @Post('reset-password') at 99 and @Public() at 100, has no SkipTenantGuard decorator, and the adapter's allowlist set was empty (security-boundary-adapter.ts:99,157,184,210). The finding is nonetheless a false positive because neither remedy the rule names corresponds to a missing control in this repository. (1) The allowlist is a per-invocation adapter input, never repository content: the tool manifest's default_input supplies only roots (security-boundary-adapter.tool.json:22) and the kernel builds the adapter payload from default_input alone (aria-kernel/aria_kernel/cycle.py:1312), so no code change can add this file to it. The repository does carry explicit records that this route is intentionally public: gateway-api's public-path list names /api/v1/auth/reset-password (tenant-context.interceptor.ts:351), and tests/invariants/platform-admin-mfa-ssot.spec.ts:16,103 records the rationale — a password reset by token has no principal — and exempts public handlers on that basis. (2) SkipTenantGuard is not this repository's tenant-skip marker for public routes. The canonical backend-common Public() already stamps SKIP_TENANT_GUARD_KEY (roles.decorator.ts:154-157), TenantGuard returns early on isPublic alone (tenant.guard.ts:106-111) and documents @SkipTenantGuard() as the marker for routes that still require authentication (tenant.guard.ts:48). admin-api-service mounts no TenantGuard: its APP_GUARDs are PlatformAdminGuard, ThrottlerGuard and PlatformCapabilityGuard (app.module.ts:371,375,384), so a @SkipTenantGuard() on this handler would have no reader. Adding it would be a decoration whose only effect is to silence the scanner, which fails the CLAUDE.md test 'if the upstream were correct, would this code need to exist?'. The co-location the adapter's fixture models (@Public() + @SkipTenantGuard(), security-boundary-adapter.test.ts:34) is what tenant-scoped services do because they mount TenantGuard (apps/sensor-service/src/app.module.ts:513; provisioning.controller.ts:40,46); the convention is guard-driven, not cosmetic. (3) The controls appropriate to a principal-less write are present and invariant-tested: @ThrottlePasswordReset() (101), @AuditedOperation (98), token verification delegated to auth-service over NATS with invalid or expired tokens rejected (110,118), no local auth-state writes (tests/invariants/tenant-provisioning-ssot.spec.ts:751), DTO validation and enumeration-safety asserted in password-reset.security.spec.ts. The endpoint is not the adapter's canonical unsafe shape — an unguarded admin mutation (UnsafePublicController.create, security-boundary-adapter.test.ts:49); a password reset cannot require authentication by construction. Root-cause fix for this false-positive class lives in the adapter (tier 3, make it detectable correctly): require the tenant-skip marker only when the unit's service mounts a TenantGuard — the adapter already collects per-service APP_GUARD names in collectServiceGuards (security-boundary-adapter.ts:249) — and treat Public as carrying tenant-skip per the canonical decorator; for a public write endpoint in a service without a tenant guard the meaningful check is the presence of throttle and audit decorators. Adjacent defect, distinct from this rule and not part of this verdict: lines 31-33 redefine IS_PUBLIC_KEY and Public locally instead of importing from apps/admin-api-service/src/decorators/public.decorator.ts:4, the key PlatformAdminGuard reads (platform-admin.guard.ts:35,156); the endpoint's public status rests on two string literals agreeing. Fix: delete the local copy, import Public from ../decorators/public.decorator, and make that file the re-export of backend-common's Public/IS_PUBLIC_KEY that the APA-371 comment at platform-admin.guard.ts:33-34 already describes.\",\n      \"evidence_refs\": [\n        \"apps/admin-api-service/src/auth/password-reset.controller.ts:98\",\n Why it matters: a wrong true_positive would push either a no-op @SkipTenantGuard() decorator (no reader in admin-api) or a scanner allowlist entry into the codebase — a patch that satisfies a tool instead of a control; a wrong false_positive on a genuinely unguarded admin mutation would leave an unauthenticated write open.
